Motivation: 量化被广泛用于压缩大语言模型以提高推理效率，通常被认为不会影响模型安全性。然而，作者发现量化可能引入新的行为偏差，尤其是在开放性生成任务中，而这类问题未被现有安全评测覆盖，因此需要专门的评估方法来揭示和衡量这一隐患。

Method: 作者设计了QuantiBias基准，包含多语言生成式刻板印象探针，并结合拒绝任务和多项选择题作为控制任务，以隔离开放性生成中的偏见表现。同时对比不同量化程度下的模型行为，评估是否进行思维链推理的影响，并使用独立评分员判断生成内容的严重性。实验覆盖两个主干模型（Qwen和Gemma）、五类家庭关系及八种语言。

Result: 实验发现，在保持模型、训练和提示不变的情况下，量化模型在标准安全测试（如拒绝有害请求、多项选择）中表现正常，但在开放性问题中显著更多地生成刻板印象，约每四次回答中就出现一次（24%~27%）。该现象在多种语言和模型中一致存在。推理前置（chain-of-thought）可使部分类别偏见减半，但对其他类别无效。不同评分者对偏见随量化加剧的趋势判断不一致。

Conclusion: 量化不仅改变模型效率，还可能损害其社会行为安全性，尤其体现在开放性生成中的隐性偏见。标准安全评估不足以保证量化后模型的安全性，必须额外进行针对开放性生成的偏见重评。QuantiBias为检测此类问题提供了有效工具，并呼吁在模型压缩流程中纳入偏见敏感的评估机制。

Abstract: Almost every large language model that reaches a broad audience is quantized: trained in full precision, then compressed for efficiency. This step is assumed harmless and its safety is rarely re-checked. We find its principal side effect is increased bias that standard safety evaluation misses. Holding the model, its training, and the prompts fixed, a quantized model still refuses harmful requests, still avoids over-refusing benign prompts, and still selects the unbiased multiple-choice answer. Yet asked an open-ended question, the same model volunteers stereotypes in all eight languages we probe, in roughly one in four open-ended answers under an independent judge (~24% to ~27% across the compression ladder): it passes every standard check and still reaches users measurably more biased. The selective gap is a robust finding; whether open-ended bias further increases with compression is less certain, sensitive to the judge that scores it. We address both with \textbf{QuantiBias}, a benchmark that pairs a generative, multilingual stereotype probe with the refusal and multiple-choice controls that isolate open-ended generation, contrasts each build with and without reasoning, and rates the content severity of what it generates. Across two backbone models (Qwen and Gemma), a five-family screen, and eight benchmarks, quantizers allocate their extra precision by capability data that carries no bias-prevention signal, and reasoning before answering roughly halves the effect on some families while doing nothing on others. A quantized build must be re-evaluated for open-ended bias, not only on the short-form safeguards it already passes.

</details>


### [149] [PrefReward: Learning User Preference Matrix for Personalized Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21067)
*Yue Wu,Chengbing Wang,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PrefReward是一种新的个性化生成框架，通过显式建模用户偏好矩阵并在解码过程中引入奖励信号，提升大语言模型在生成质量和个性化可解释性方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化生成方法多依赖隐式表示，难以解释用户偏好且处理长上下文能力有限，因此需要一种更清晰、可控的个性化建模方式。

Method: 提出PrefReward框架，包含两个阶段：首先提取用户特定的偏好矩阵以总结风格倾向，然后在解码时利用基于KL散度的奖励函数将该矩阵作为奖励信号指导生成过程。

Result: 在LongLaMP数据集上的实验表明，PrefReward在生成质量和个性化可解释性方面优于非个性化和基于检索的基线方法。

Conclusion: PrefReward通过显式的偏好矩阵建模有效提升了个性化文本生成的效果与透明度，为可控风格生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable ability in generating personalized content by leveraging user histories and contextual cues. However, most existing personalization approaches rely on implicit representations within model parameters, making it difficult to interpret user-specific preferences or effectively handle long-context dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose PrefReward, a novel preference-aware generative framework that explicitly models user styles through a structured preference matrix and integrates it into the decoding process as a reward signal. PrefReward consists of two stages: (1) extracting a user-specific preference matrix that summarizes individual stylistic tendencies, and (2) using the matrix to guide generation via a KL-divergence-based reward function. Experiments on the LongLaMP dataset show that PrefReward outperforms non-personalized and retrieval-based baselines in both generation quality and personalization interpretability.

</details>


### [150] [One More Turn, Less Regret: A Regret-Based Multi-Turn Benchmark for LLMs' Clarification Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21143)
*Minh Ngoc Ta,My Anh Tran Nguyen,Duong D. Nguyen,Yuxia Wang,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RegretBench是一个用于评估对话式LLM助手中澄清行为的多轮基准，通过衡量意图解析、交互成本和遗憾程度，揭示模型是否以有用且高效的方式进行澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊的用户请求使得澄清成为对话系统中的序贯决策问题，现有方法仅关注单个问题质量，缺乏对整体澄清策略的评估。

Method: 提出RegretBench，采用隐藏意图设定，支持基于语义状态跟踪的自由交互，并引入基于遗憾的目标函数，衡量模型相对于参考策略的性能损失。

Result: 实验表明，在开放域问答和商品推荐中，仅看最终准确率不足以评估澄清效果，不同模型在效率、鲁棒性和停止决策上差异显著；RegretBench能有效区分澄清策略的优劣。

Conclusion: 有效的澄清不仅需要提出合理的问题，还需在正确的时间提问并在意图明确时及时停止，强调了策略性决策的重要性。

Abstract: Ambiguous user requests make clarification a sequential decision problem for conversational LLM assistants: they must decide whether to ask, what to ask, when to stop, and when to answer. We introduce RegretBench, a multi-turn benchmark that evaluates clarification as policy behavior rather than isolated question quality. RegretBench provides a hidden-intent formulation of ambiguity, supports free-form interaction grounded in semantic-state tracking, and introduces a regret-based objective that measures how much value a model loses relative to a reference clarification policy. Experiments on open-domain QA and product recommendation scenarios show that final success alone is insufficient, as models with similar accuracy can differ substantially in efficiency, robustness to user behaviors, and stopping decisions. By jointly measuring intent resolution, interaction cost, ineffective clarification, and regret, RegretBench reveals whether models clarify usefully and efficiently. Our results show that effective clarification requires more than plausible questions: models must ask the right question at the right time and stop once the user's intended meaning is clear.

</details>


### [151] [Progressive Cramming: Reliable Token Compression and What It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21231)
*Dmitrii Tarasov,Timofei Lashukov,Elizaveta Goncharova,Andrey Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了渐进式压缩（progressive cramming）方法，用于研究序列压缩的极限。与传统方法不同，该方法逐个增加目标前缀长度，直到无法在固定优化预算内实现重构为止。实验发现，渐进式压缩轨迹在嵌入空间中具有低维结构，并且压缩嵌入会影响模型在多选和生成任务上的表现，主要归因于早期层中的注意力交互。结果表明，完美重构并不足以实现有意义的压缩，因其依赖脆弱的引导而非可迁移的语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token压缩方法受限于固定的token预算和99%准确率阈值，难以判断重构误差是源于优化失败还是本质限制。因此需要一种新方法来更系统地探索压缩的可行性边界。

Method: 提出渐进式压缩方法：逐步增长目标前缀长度，每步尝试在其上实现输入序列的近似重构，若在给定优化预算内失败则停止。分析压缩轨迹在嵌入空间的几何特性，并通过插入压缩嵌入进行下游任务评估及因果干预分析其影响机制。

Result: 1. 渐进式压缩轨迹呈现出低维结构；2. 插入压缩嵌入导致多选基准准确率一致下降，在生成任务中几乎完全丧失能力；3. 因果干预显示性能下降源于嵌入在模型早期层中的注意力干扰效应；4. 完美重构可通过高度敏感的‘脆性引导’实现，但不传递语义信息。

Conclusion: 渐进式压缩是一种有效探究语言模型中序列压缩极限的新工具。研究揭示了完美重构并非等同于有效压缩，强调了可迁移语义的重要性，指出当前方法可能过度依赖对模型内部表示的非鲁棒操控。

Abstract: Token cramming compresses sequences into learned embeddings with near-perfect reconstruction, but fixed token budgets and 99\% accuracy thresholds leave it unclear whether residual errors reflect optimization failures or fundamental limits. We introduce progressive cramming, which grows the target prefix token-by-token, stopping only when reconstruction is no longer achievable within a fixed optimization budget. Progressive trajectories occupy low-dimensional structure in embedding space. Prepending a crammed embedding causes a moderate but consistent accuracy drop on multiple-choice benchmarks even with the original prefix in context, and collapses capability almost entirely under generative evaluation. Causal attention-knockout interventions trace this degradation to the embedding's interactions in the model's early layers. These results position progressive cramming as a tool for studying compression limits and show that perfect reconstruction - achievable through brittle steering rather than transferable semantics - is insufficient for meaningful compression.

</details>


### [152] [slang.gr as a Large-Scale Crowdsourced Resource for Non-Standard Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21255)
*Panagiotis Papadakos,Katerina Papantoniou,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了对希腊语非标准语言词典slang.gr的首次大规模计算研究，通过构建结构化多层分类法分析俚语的语言结构和贡献者社区行为，并引入基于社区的信任评分机制，为社会语言学NLP、偏见分析和LLM中非正式语言研究提供了新基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于俚语具有动态性和非标准性，难以进行计算建模，因此需要系统性的方法来理解和分析非标准语言及其社会语言学特征。

Method: 将嘈杂的用户生成标签映射到一个结构化的多层分类体系，结合词汇内容和互动数据，分析希腊俚语的语言结构和用户行为，并提出一种融合用户角色、互动模式和审核信号的基于社区的信任评分模型。

Result: 发现希腊俚语高度集中于与人相关和评价性语言，表现出强烈的形态创造性，用户参与度高度偏斜且生命周期短，存在重叠社区；分类法表示提升了信任信号的可解释性并保留了行为结构信息。

Conclusion: slang.gr可作为非标准希腊语的重要计算资源，所提出的分类框架和信任评分机制为社会语言学NLP和大型语言模型中的非正式语言研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Slang is a central component of everyday language, reflecting linguistic creativity, social identity, and cultural change, yet its dy- namic and non-standard nature makes it difficult to model computationally. We present the first large-scale computational study of slang.gr, a crowdsourced lexicon of Greek non-standard language, combining lexical content, user-generated tags, and interaction data. To enable the systematic analysis, we map noisy folksonomic tags to a structured multi-layer taxonomy capturing both semantic categories and sociolinguistic metadata. Using this representation, we analyze the linguistic structure of Greek slang and the behavior of its contributor community. We find that slang is strongly centered on person-related and evaluative language, exhibits high morphological creativity, and is shaped by highly skewed participation with short user lifespans and overlapping communities. Building on these signals, we introduce a community-based confidence score for definitions that integrates user roles, interaction patterns, and moderation signals. Our results show that taxonomy-based representations improve interpretability while retaining meaningful aspects of behavioral structure, enabling a more structured and interpretable analysis of confidence signals. Overall, this work establishes slang.gr as a computational resource for non-standard Greek and provides a foundation for sociolinguistic NLP, bias analysis, and the study of informal language in LLMs.

</details>


### [153] [A Comparative Evaluation of Embeddings and LLMs in a Greek Book Publisher Setting - The CUP Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21274)
*Katerina Papantoniou,Panagiotis Papadakos,Theodore Patkos,Dimitris Garefalakis,Nikos Vardakis,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CUP是一个希腊语图书检索基准，包含868条目录记录和104个专家标注的带分级相关性判断的查询，用于评估多种检索方法在真实场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了推动希腊语文本检索技术的发展，填补非英语、尤其是低资源语言在图书检索领域的评估空白，需要一个专门针对希腊语的高质量基准数据集。

Method: 构建了CUP基准，包括目录数据和人工标注查询；评估了稀疏（BM25）、密集（sentence-transformers）、混合及LLM辅助检索方法；进行了查询级别的细粒度分析，并测试了字段感知提示、LLM目录摘要和LLM后过滤等策略。

Result: 多语言嵌入模型优于希腊语专用模型；混合检索整体表现最佳；BM25在命名实体查询上表现好，而密集与混合方法在自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询上更优；字段感知提示效果因模型而异，LLM生成目录摘要可提升仅目录检索效果，LLM后过滤能改善早期检索性能但成本高。

Conclusion: CUP为希腊语图书检索提供了真实可靠的评估平台，支持对词汇、语义、噪声和跨语言等多种查询类型的综合评价，推动了低资源语言信息检索的发展。

Abstract: We present CUP, a Greek book retrieval benchmark consisting of 868 catalog records and 104 expert-annotated queries with graded relevance judgments. We evaluate sparse (BM25), dense (sentence-transformers), hybrid, and LLM-assisted retrieval methods in this book-search setting. Multilingual embeddings outperform Greek-specific models, while hybrid retrieval performs best overall. A query-level analysis shows that BM25 excels at named-entity queries, while dense and hybrid methods improve natural-language, noisy, cross-lingual, and concept queries. Field-aware prompting has model-specific effects, while LLM TOC summarization improves TOC-only retrieval and LLM post-filtering improves early-stage retrieval at a high cost. Overall, CUP enables real-world evaluation of Greek retrieval across lexical, semantic, noisy, and cross-lingual queries.

</details>


### [154] [A Unified Moral-Value Dataset for Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21279)
*Zhaohui Zeng,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于指令调优的统一道德价值数据集，旨在解决大语言模型的价值对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的指令调优数据集在零样本任务中表现出潜力，但缺乏专门针对道德情境和行为设计的数据集，因此需要构建一个专注于道德价值的数据集以促进价值对齐研究。

Method: 通过整合现有的道德价值数据集，将其合并为统一语料库，并转换为指令-响应格式，从而构建一个新的道德价值数据集；同时将该数据集与通用任务数据集混合进行训练，评估其对通用任务性能和价值导向任务性能的影响。

Result: 实验证明，在混合数据集上训练能够保持通用任务的性能，并初步观察到不同混合比例对价值导向任务性能的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提供的道德价值数据集可直接用于指令调优，为大语言模型的价值对齐研究提供了有用的资源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have developed rapidly and become valuable tools in everyday life. However, how to align LLMs to a particular set of human values is still an open problem. Recent studies show that instruction tuning has strong potential for zero-shot tasks and may serve as an effective approach to addressing value alignment. Nevertheless, although many datasets for instruction tuning already exist, they are not specifically designed around moral scenarios and behaviors. We construct a unified moral-value dataset that can be directly used for instruction tuning. This dataset is built upon existing moral-value datasets by merging them into a unified corpus and converting them into an instruction-response format. We show that training on a mixed dataset combining general task datasets with our dataset preserves general-task performance, and we report preliminary observations on how the mixing ratio affects value-oriented task performance. Our work provides a moral-value dataset for instruction tuning and offers a useful resource for further alignment research. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/teohzzh/value-for-instruction-tuning.

</details>


### [155] [news-crawler-LM: A Small Long-Context Model For High-Quality News Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21284)
*Pascal Stolzenburg,Jonas Golde,Max Dallabetta,Alan Akbik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为news-crawler-LM的小型长上下文语言模型，用于从新闻网页中提取结构化内容，相较于强基线模型在HTML到Markdown和HTML到JSON的转换任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于网页HTML布局异构、标记不一致以及存在大量模板内容（如导航栏和广告），从新闻页面提取结构化内容具有挑战性；传统基于规则的方法需要手动配置以适应新网站，而大语言模型虽灵活但计算成本高。

Method: 通过在高质量、人工验证的Fundus新闻爬取库数据上微调小型长上下文语言模型，实现将原始HTML转换为纯文本和包含标题、作者、发布日期及正文等字段的结构化JSON。

Result: 实验显示，news-crawler-LM在HTML-to-Markdown任务中BLEU提升+4.8、METEOR提升+6.1，在HTML-to-JSON任务中BLEU提升+2.2、METEOR提升+4.1；但在HTML-to-plaintext任务中对未见发布者的改进有限。

Conclusion: news-crawler-LM在结构化信息提取方面优于现有方法，尤其在生成结构化输出时表现突出，但其在纯文本提取上的泛化能力仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Extracting structured content from news pages remains challenging due to heterogeneous HTML layouts, inconsistent markup, and substantial boilerplate such as navigation elements and advertisements. Rule-based news crawlers can achieve high extraction accuracy by encoding site-specific structure, but require manual configuration in order to generalize to new publishers. Large language models provide a more flexible alternative by reducing the need for handcrafted rules, but their high computational cost limits practical deployment. In this paper, we introduce news-crawler-LM, a small long-context language model fine-tuned on high-quality, human-validated extractions from the Fundus news-crawling library. Our model converts raw HTML into plaintext and structured JSON, including fields such as headline, author, publication date, and article body. In our experiments, news-crawler-LM outperforms strong baselines in HTML-to-Markdown and HTML-to-JSON extraction, improving performance by +4.8 BLEU and +6.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-Markdown task, and by +2.2 BLEU and +4.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-JSON task. However, we also observe that our model only slightly better compared to other rule-based parsing libraries on the HTML-to-plaintext task in evaluations on previously unseen publishers. We release all models and artifacts to the research community.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework: Similarity-Driven Resource Allocation for Pre-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21291)
*Yidu Wu,Xiang Wang,Kejie Zhao,Zhangchi Wang,Qinghai Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需完全重训练的自适应深度稀疏框架AdaDSF，用于降低大语言模型推理成本，同时保持接近全模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速方法依赖任务特定微调或从头训练，增加适配成本并限制跨任务应用，因此需要一种通用且高效的推理加速方法。

Method: 基于层输入输出隐藏状态的余弦相似度分析层贡献度，据此分配每层的令牌保留比例；使用轻量级路由器选择关键令牌，并通过特征保持对齐目标匹配稀疏与稠密模型的中间及最终表示。

Result: 在GPT-NeoX和Qwen2.5上验证了AdaDSF在语言建模与常识推理任务中显著减少推理FLOPs的同时，性能接近稠密模型，且优于MoD、D-LLM等基线方法。

Conclusion: AdaDSF能有效实现预训练大模型的深度稀疏化推理，兼顾效率与性能，具备良好的跨模型与任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong generation and reasoning performance, but the Transformer architecture incurs high inference cost. Existing acceleration methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or training from scratch, increasing adaptation cost and limiting cross-task usability. We present an Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework (AdaDSF) that converts off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs into depth-sparse models without full retraining. Our key insight is that layers contribute unequally to representation transformation, characterized by the cosine similarity between layer input and output hidden states. Based on this, AdaDSF assigns layer-wise token retention ratios from similarity statistics, uses a lightweight router to select informative tokens at each layer, and introduces a feature-preserving alignment objective to match intermediate and final representations between sparse and dense models. On GPT-NeoX and Qwen2.5 over language modeling and commonsense reasoning, AdaDSF substantially reduces inference FLOPs while preserving performance close to dense counterparts. Under comparable sparsity, AdaDSF consistently yields smaller accuracy degradation than strong baselines including MoD, D-LLM, and DLO.

</details>


### [157] [GRADRAG: Cross-Component Prompt Adaptation for Coordinated Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21324)
*Paolo Pedinotti,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRADRAG是一个用于检索增强生成（RAG）系统的跨组件提示优化框架，通过计算图建模和结构化反馈传播，提升多代理协作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常孤立优化各组件，缺乏对整个流程的协同改进，限制了系统整体性能。

Method: 将RAG流程建模为计算图，引入Evaluator对下游输出进行评估并生成结构化反馈，由Prompt Optimizer利用该反馈迭代优化上游代理（如检索器、图构建器和回答器），并支持评估驱动的早停机制。

Result: 在SQUALITY和QMSUM基准上，结合两种检索范式（基于chunk的检索与基于图的检索），GRADRAG在LLM评判的成对比较中比单步优化基线高出12-15个百分点，且多数提升在两次迭代内完成。

Conclusion: GRADRAG通过跨组件协同优化显著提升了RAG系统的性能，验证了全局反馈驱动提示调优的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly employ multiple LLM agents. Yet, most prior work optimizes components in isolation rather than coordinating improvements across the pipeline. We introduce GRADRAG, a framework for cross-component prompt adaptation that models the RAG pipeline as a computational graph and propagates structured evaluation feedback to update upstream agents. An Evaluator critiques downstream answers and supporting evidence, producing actionable feedback that a Prompt Optimizer uses to iteratively update adaptive agents, such as retrievers, graph constructors, and answerers. The Evaluator also triggers early stopping when the output is deemed satisfactory. We evaluate GRADRAG on the SQUALITY and QMSUM benchmarks under two retrieval paradigms: flat chunk-based retrieval using IRCoT-style query refinement (Trivedi et al., 2023), and graph-based retrieval that constructs and iteratively enriches an entity-relation graph from the document. Across both settings, GRADRAG consistently outperforms one-step refinement baselines that update only the final generator, achieving a 12-15 percentage point net preference margin in LLM-judged pairwise comparisons, with most gains realized within two refinement iterations.

</details>


### [158] [Phonetic forced alignment for low-resource language varieties: Model training and evaluation on Chengdu Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21332)
*Zhiheng Qian,Aini Li,Hai Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对资源较少的成都普通话方言的语音强制对齐方法，通过构建文本相关和文本无关的对齐系统，显著优于标准普通话基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音对齐系统缺乏针对低资源语言变体的专用模型，限制了方言语音研究的准确性与效率。

Method: 使用17小时语料库和自定义G2P词典训练文本相关的GMM-HMM模型（Chengdu-MFA），并利用其伪标签微调预训练音频编码器以实现文本无关对齐（Chengdu-FC）。

Result: 在专家标注测试集上，Chengdu-MFA使平均音素边界差异减少31.8%，Chengdu-FC减少61.2%，均显著优于标准普通话基线。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个实用的引导流程，可用于为资源不足的语言变体开发高精度对齐工具，无需耗时费力的手动标注。

Abstract: Phonetic forced alignment is a key technique in phonetic research, yet existing alignment systems lack specialized models for low-resource language varieties. We address this by training text-dependent and text-independent aligners for Chengdu Mandarin using a 17-hour corpus and a custom G2P dictionary. We trained a text-dependent GMM-HMM model (Chengdu-MFA) and fine-tuned a pretrained audio encoder on frame classification with Chengdu-MFA's pseudo label for text-independent alignment (Chengdu-FC). Evaluation on an expert-annotated test set show that both methods significantly outperform Standard Mandarin baselines. Chengdu-MFA reduced average phone boundary differences by 31.8%, while Chengdu-FC achieved a 61.2% reduction. This work establishes a practical bootstrapping pipeline for developing accurate aligners for under-resourced varieties without labor- and time-intensive manual annotation.

</details>


### [159] [Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score (CM-LRS): From Plausible to Bankable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21340)
*Prerit Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CM-LRS——一种用于评估资本市场中大语言模型输出可靠性的多维评分体系，聚焦于实际工作流中的可辩护性（即“可兑现性”），并在五个真实金融任务上验证了其有效性，发现顶尖闭源模型表现接近，而开源模型仍有差距，尤其在信息检索与综合能力方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评估方法多集中于问答层面的准确性，缺乏对资本市场实际工作流中输出内容是否具备可审计、可追溯、可辩护等关键属性的系统评估，难以满足监管和实务需求。

Method: 提出CM-LRS评分框架，涵盖事实准确性、证据可追溯性、数值一致性、流程完整性、来源纪律性、决策有用性和可审阅性七个维度，每项0-5分；基于SEC文件、公开并购公告及合成数据，在五个资本市场监管相关任务上评估四种模型，并由四个独立LLM评审员打分。

Result: 1）顶级闭源模型（Sonnet、Opus、GPT-5.5）在CM-LRS总分上表现接近（4.31–4.09），开源Llama 3.3 70B得分最低（3.15）；2）性能差距主要体现在检索（2.23）和综合（2.15）任务，而非提取任务（0.84）；3）决策有用性维度表现出最大的模型间差异（达4.0分）和较高的评审者间一致性（r=0.52）。

Conclusion: 语言模型生成内容的表面合理性已不再稀缺，真正关键的是其在受监管环境下的‘可兑现性’；CM-LRS提供了一个贴近实践的评估标准，有助于推动更可靠、可审计的AI系统在资本市场的应用。

Abstract: In capital-markets workflows the question is rarely whether a large language model can produce a fluent draft, but whether the draft is bankable: defensible in front of a counter-party or a regulator, with the documents in hand. Existing methods address parts of that gap: open-domain QA benchmarks reward surface accuracy, and finance benchmarks (FinanceBench, FinQA, ConvFinQA) advance document-grounded and numerical QA but evaluate at the question-answer layer rather than the workflow outputs practitioners defend.
  We introduce CM-LRS, a Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score, evaluating outputs at the workflow-output layer across seven dimensions: factual accuracy, evidence traceability, numerical consistency, workflow completeness, source discipline, decision usefulness, and reviewability/auditability. Each is scored 0-5 against a rubric anchored on signals reviewers in regulated settings use; the aggregate is tunable to the workflow.
  We demonstrate CM-LRS on five workflows (DCM transaction-terms extraction, precedent retrieval, issuer profile synthesis, M&A transaction-comparable reasoning, ECM transaction-terms extraction) over public SEC EDGAR filings, a public UK takeover release, and fictional synthetic supplements, scoring four models against four independent LLM judges spanning three model families.
  Three findings. First, the frontier closed-source models cluster within 0.22 points on four-judge averaged CM-LRS (Sonnet 4.6 = 4.31, Opus 4.7 = 4.30, GPT-5.5 = 4.09); all four judges place the open-weights baseline (Llama 3.3 70B = 3.15) last. Second, that gap concentrates on retrieval (2.23) and synthesis (2.15), not extraction (0.84). Third, Decision Usefulness shows the widest cross-model dispersion of any dimension (4.0 points on issuer profiling) and top-tier inter-judge agreement (mean r = 0.52).
  Plausibility is cheap. Bankability is the bar.

</details>


### [160] [Word meaning co-determines vowel-inherent spectral change. A corpus-based investigation of conversational Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21391)
*Xiaoyun Jin,Mirjam Ernestus,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现，在控制多种变量后，汉语口语中元音的共振峰动态具有与词义相关的特定成分，语义信息影响发音细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究汉语口语中元音固有频谱变化（VISC）是否受语义影响，挑战言语产生中的模块化认知模型。

Method: 采用广义加性模型和基于分布语义的词嵌入方法，分析自发对话中的元音共振峰轨迹。

Result: 在控制元音时长、性别、说话人身份等因素后，词的F1和F2轨迹可通过其上下文化词嵌入显著预测，准确率高于置换基线。

Conclusion: 词汇的语义共同决定了其发音的细微特征，支持语义与发音在言语产出中紧密耦合的观点。

Abstract: This study investigates vowel-inherent spectral change (VISC) in spontaneous conversational Mandarin. Using the generalized additive model and word embeddings from distributional semantics, we show that, when controlling for variables such as vowel duration, gender, speaker identity, co-articulation, vowel identity, and utterance position, vowel formant trajectory dynamics have word-specific components that are tied to their meaning in context: The F1 and F2 trajectories of words can be predicted from their contextualized embeddings with an accuracy that substantially exceeds a permutation baseline. Challenging modular cognitive models of speech production, these results indicate that, words' semantics co-determine the fine details of their articulation.

</details>


### [161] [MemTools: A Unified Research Framework for Interoperable Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21404)
*Chengfeng Zhao,Jinhui Chen,Sirui Liang,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemTools是一个解耦内存系统组件与其部署环境的互操作性研究框架，通过声明式数据合约标准化内存生命周期，支持跨系统组件组合、评估协议重构和异构内存协调，促进智能体记忆的系统化研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的内存系统实现通常将内存生命周期的不同阶段耦合在一起，纠缠了特定数据集的评估逻辑，并且对异构内存类型的支持有限，阻碍了系统性的研究。因此需要一个能够解耦组件、统一接口并支持多样化内存表示的框架。

Method: 提出MemTools框架，采用声明式数据合约来标准化内存生命周期；正交分离基准数据集与执行协议；提供统一计算接口以协调符号、神经及多模态内存表示，并在共享运行时中进行集成。

Result: 实验评估表明，MemTools支持跨系统组件集成、评估协议重新配置以及异构内存协调，能够实现对内存设计变量的系统性隔离与分析。

Conclusion: MemTools为推进基于原则的智能体记忆研究提供了实用且可扩展的基础设施，有助于克服当前内存系统碎片化带来的研究限制。

Abstract: While memory systems are essential for agent architectures, pervasive architectural fragmentation restricts systematic research. Existing implementations typically couple different stages of the memory lifecycle, entangle evaluation logic with specific datasets, and provide limited support for the management of heterogeneous memory types. We introduce MemTools, an interoperability research framework that decouples memory system components from their underlying deployment environments. MemTools standardizes the memory lifecycle through declarative data contracts, enabling the interchangeable assembly of components across different systems. It orthogonally separates benchmark datasets from execution protocols to facilitate controlled assessments. Furthermore, MemTools provides a unified computational interface for coordinating symbolic, neural, and multimodal memory representations within a shared runtime. Empirical evaluations on cross-system component integration, evaluation protocol reconfiguration, and heterogeneous memory coordination demonstrate that MemTools enables systematic isolation and analysis of memory design variables. These findings suggest that MemTools provides a practical and extensible infrastructure for advancing principled research on agent memory.

</details>


### [162] [Anti-Periodic Positional Encoding: Möbius Boundary Conditions Make In-Context Retrieval Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21405)
*Ji Ho Bae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Möbius RoPE是一种基于反周期频率梯子的旋转位置编码，通过引入反周期边界条件，显著提升了长程依赖下的针尖检索可靠性，且不增加困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的位置编码在处理长序列时存在长程依赖建模不足的问题，尤其是对序列两端的耦合缺乏明确机制；本文旨在提出一种新的位置编码方法，以改善模型在长上下文中的信息检索稳定性。

Method: 提出Möbius RoPE，采用反周期频率梯子θ_i=π(2i+1)/N，使每个旋转平面在训练上下文中前进奇数倍π，实现位置全纯性为-1，并通过闭式Dirichlet‘偶极’边界耦合序列两端；通过预训练48个模型（不同规模和结构）进行验证，包括混合架构（25%注意力头使用Möbius频率），并与标准RoPE、周期性和非周期性变体进行对照实验。

Result: 混合模型困惑度保持不变（29.66 vs 29.72），但在512上下文长度下针尖检索准确率显著提升至90.3±5.7%（对照组63.3±31.4%），最差种子表现从14%提升至86%，方差显著降低（p<0.03）；效果在410M模型中复现（Levene p=0.040）；控制实验显示仅反周期结构有效，替换回标准RoPE导致远距离针尖检索崩溃；NoPE虽短程可靠但困惑度上升13%且外推性能差。

Conclusion: Möbius RoPE是首个引入反周期边界条件的位置编码，能稳定长程依赖建模、消除种子敏感性，同时保持语言建模性能，为Transformer的位置编码设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Möbius RoPE is a rotary positional encoding built on the anti-periodic frequency ladder $θ_i=π(2i+1)/N$: every rotation plane advances by an odd multiple of $π$ across the training context, so the positional holonomy is $-1$ and the two ends of the sequence are deterministically coupled through a closed-form Dirichlet "dipole"; to our knowledge this is the first anti-periodic boundary condition in positional encoding. We verify the theory numerically to $\sim 10^{-6}$ and pretrain 48 models spanning six 160M-class and three 410M-class arms (2B FineWeb-Edu tokens each; the hybrid arm puts Möbius frequencies on 25% of heads). Hybrid perplexity is unchanged (29.66 vs. 29.72), but needle-in-a-haystack retrieval becomes reliable: $90.3\pm5.7\%$ versus $63.3\pm31.4\%$ at context 512 ($n=6$ seeds), observed worst seed 86% versus 14%, robust variance tests $p=0.013$-$0.029$ (unadjusted), recurring at 410M (Levene $p=0.040$). Matched controls isolate the mechanism: an aperiodic ladder in the same frequency band reproduces none of the effect, and a periodic (holonomy $+1$) ladder only a fraction. Swapping trained models' frequency table back to standard RoPE (weights frozen) collapses retrieval, with damage concentrated on far needles: trained models depend on this long-range geometry. A NoPE arm is even more reliable at short context but pays a 13% perplexity tax and extrapolates worst; only the anti-periodic hybrid pairs baseline perplexity with a high reliability floor. The effect is scoped to single-needle retrieval within the training window; a one-line frequency swap thus provides zero-cost insurance against the retrieval seed lottery.

</details>


### [163] [An Evaluation Framework for Structured Audio Captions Validated by Controlled Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21424)
*Liang-Yuan Wu,Sripathi Sridhar,Mark Cartwright,Magdalena Fuentes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对结构化音频描述的多轴评估框架，结合大语言模型评判和计算指标，有效区分语义保持改写与真实错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动音频描述评价方法主要面向扁平文本，难以准确评估包含多种模态属性的结构化输出，缺乏可靠的多维度评估手段。

Method: 基于AudioCards数据集，从标签集、描述、逻辑推理、数值测量和频谱特征五个正交维度构建评估框架，结合大语言模型评判语义细微差异，并使用确定性计算指标精确衡量声学偏差。

Result: 通过引入可控的分级错误扰动测试协议，验证了该框架能有效区分意义保持的同义改写与真正的语义或声学损坏。

Conclusion: 所提出的多轴评估框架能够更全面、可靠地评估结构化音频描述，为未来自动化音频描述系统的发展提供了新的评测基准。

Abstract: Recent advancements in automated audio captioning (AAC) have shifted from monolithic sentence generation toward structured formats that explicitly disentangle distinct acoustic and semantic properties. However, evaluating this heterogeneous data remains a significant challenge. Existing caption metrics focus on flat textual outputs and fail to reliably assess multimodal attributes. To bridge this gap, we propose a multi-axis evaluation framework tailored for structured audio descriptions. Building on the AudioCards dataset, we evaluate outputs across five orthogonal axes: tag-sets, descriptions, logical reasoning, numeric measurements, and spectral profiles. Our approach combines Large Language Model (LLM) judges to capture semantic nuance with deterministic computational metrics to precisely measure acoustic deviations. To rigorously validate the reliability of this framework, we introduce a controlled perturbation testing protocol that injects typed, graded errors into groundtruth annotations. Our results demonstrate that this framework successfully distinguishes meaning-preserving paraphrases from genuine semantic and acoustic corruptions.

</details>


### [164] [Token Budget Saturation and Mechanistic Early Detection of Reasoning Non-Convergence in Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21433)
*Renuka Oladri,Niveda Jawahar,Abdirisak Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了思维链推理模型的双峰收敛模式，发现收敛生成在AIME上准确率达90.3%，而非收敛仅为6.6%。通过训练线性探针分析隐藏状态，发现早期中间表征可预测生成是否收敛，为提前退出推理和自适应计算分配提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解思维链推理模型中为何部分生成能收敛而另一些不能，并探索是否能在生成早期预测其收敛性，以提升推理效率和资源分配。

Method: 基于DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B模型，分析不同生成结果的隐藏状态激活情况，使用线性探针在token位置50-300的层激活上进行训练，并与基于熵和重复率的行为基线比较，采用交叉验证和置换检验评估显著性。

Result: 收敛生成准确率为90.3%，非收敛为6.6%，总体收敛率为62.0%；第20层在token 150处的激活预测收敛性的AUC为0.608（±0.080），显著高于随机水平；置换检验p=0.063，激活探针优于行为基线。

Conclusion: 思维链生成的收敛命运在早期中间表示中已有部分编码，表明可通过内部激活实现早期预测，支持未来开发早停机制和动态计算资源分配策略。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B exhibit a bimodal convergence pattern: generations either terminate within a token budget (converged) or exhaust it without reaching a conclusion (non-converged). We characterize this phenomenon empirically, showing that converged generations achieve 90.3% accuracy on AIME 1983-2024 while non-converged ones achieve only 6.6%, with an overall convergence rate of 62.0%. We then ask whether this outcome is detectable early in the thinking chain using internal model representations. Training linear probes on hidden-state activations at token positions 50-300, we find that layer-20 activations at token 150 achieve AUC 0.608 (+-0.080, 5-fold CV), reliably above chance even at token 50. Activation probes consistently outperform behavioral baselines derived from token entropy and repetition statistics. A sweep-level permutation test yields p=0.063 (100,000 permutations), consistent with a modest signal that our sample size cannot confirm at conventional thresholds. These findings suggest that convergence fate is partially encoded in intermediate representations well before the generation ends, opening a path toward early-exit inference and adaptive compute allocation.

</details>


### [165] [When Trivia Is Not Trivial: Everyday Knowledge Failures in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21445)
*Anna Mosolova,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为TriviaRoomQA的多语言基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在日常、文化相关和长尾知识领域的表现，发现现有模型在历史、地理等知识密集型主题上表现良好，但在流行文化等日常主题上较弱，且性能因语言而异，揭示了当前学术基准未能捕捉到的重要知识差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大语言模型是否能在涵盖广泛主题的问答场景中表现出竞争力，并检验其在常见与小众话题上的知识覆盖能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含3,300个六种欧洲语言平行多项选择题和额外5,340个法语题目的多语言基准TriviaRoomQA，涵盖288个主题，并对来自欧美亚的30个开源大语言模型（7B至70B参数）进行评估。

Result: 模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型领域表现强，但在名人、音乐、电影、新闻等日常流行文化领域明显较弱；相同问题在不同语言下模型表现存在差异，表明事实知识获取并非完全语言无关。

Conclusion: 现有大语言模型在日常文化和多语言知识掌握方面存在显著短板，TriviaRoomQA揭示了当前饱和学术基准未能反映的重要知识缺口。

Abstract: Quiz rooms, trivia nights, and quiz shows challenge human knowledge across a wide range of topics, from canonical facts to everyday culture. In this paper, we examine whether large language models (LLMs) can perform competitively in such settings, using quiz-style questions to test them on both common and niche topics. We introduce TriviaRoomQA, a multilingual benchmark designed to evaluate everyday, culturally grounded, and long-tail knowledge across 288 topics. The benchmark contains 3,300 parallel multiple-choice questions in six European languages and additional 5,340 French-only questions for a more fine-grained case study. We evaluate 30 open-weight LLMs from European, Asian, and North American providers, covering models from 7 to 70B parameters. We find that models are strong on knowledge-intensive topics such as history, geography, and mathematics, but substantially weaker on everyday popular-culture topics such as celebrities, music, movies, and news. Moreover, model performance varies across languages even for the same underlying questions, suggesting that access to factual knowledge is not always language-independent. In sum, our dataset and experiments demonstrate an important knowledge gap which is not captured by existing academic-based saturated benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [RUMBA: Russian User Memory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21447)
*Elizaveta Shevtsova,Inna Glebkina,Mark Baushenko,Pavel Gulyaev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RUMBA是一个面向俄语的长期对话记忆基准，包含细粒度问题分类和统一评估方法，支持跨会话的记忆检索与推理，并提供英语对照子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评测基准以英语为中心且依赖聚合检索指标，难以捕捉长距离上下文、时间信息与推理之间的交互。

Method: 提出RUMBA基准，包含带时间戳的用户-助手对话及需跨会话检索与推理的问答对，构建涵盖语义类型、会话范围、时间推理和时间表达显式性的统一评估框架。

Result: RUMBA能够作为诊断工具分析模型在不同切片上的表现，揭示不同记忆机制的优势与失效模式。

Conclusion: RUMBA为多语言长期记忆评估提供了更细粒度、更系统的评测方案，有助于深入理解模型的记忆行为。

Abstract: The ability to handle long-term memory in LLMs is becoming increasingly critical, yet existing benchmarks remain English-centric and rely on aggregate retrieval metrics, failing to capture interactions between long-range context, temporal information, and reasoning. To address this, we introduce RUMBA (Russian User Memory BenchmArk) - a new benchmark for long-term conversational memory that provides a fine-grained taxonomy of memory-centric question types and a unified methodology accounting for semantic type, session scope, temporal reasoning, and the explicitness of temporal expressions. RUMBA consists of timestamped user-assistant dialogues with QA pairs requiring retrieval, combination, and reasoning across sessions. While designed for Russian, we also provide an aligned English subset under the same methodology. We evaluate contemporary memory systems and long-context models, and show how RUMBA serves as a diagnostic tool to analyze model behavior across benchmark slices and identify strengths and failure modes of different memory mechanisms.

</details>


### [167] [What, Where, and How: Disentangling the Roles of Task, Language, and Model in Code Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21491)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了不同语言模型在处理编程语言（Python和Rust）时对语法概念的表征一致性，发现模型间在哪些概念被专门电路化上高度一致，但在电路位置和跨层增长模式上因模型而异，揭示了表征内容具有普遍性而计算组织结构不具备普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究独立训练的语言模型是否以相同方式表示相同内容，特别是在代码理解任务中，分析任务、语言和模型架构各自对神经表征的影响。

Method: 扩展了一种概念-电路提取方法，采用2x2实验设计（两种语言：Python、Rust；两种模型：Qwen2.5-Coder-7B、DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B），系统测量58个Python和57个Rust语法概念在四个条件下的神经表征，并通过相关性分析、消融实验和线性探针验证功能。

Result: 模型在‘什么’概念获得专用电路方面高度一致（Spearman ρ≈0.65，p<1e-7），但‘电路位置’和‘跨层增长模式’由具体模型决定（Qwen在L17-19，DeepSeek在L6-7）；Rust比Python获得更多专用电路；两模型均共享跨语言神经元（DeepSeek更多）；Qwen将Rust类型与trait关键词聚类于同一神经元群（Jaccard=0.535），且功能验证证实这些电路有效。

Conclusion: ‘电路是否通用’无单一答案：内容层面（What）具有跨模型普遍性，但组织层面（Where和How）由模型自身决定，表明表征的普遍性存在于语义内容而非计算结构中。

Abstract: Do independently trained language models come to represent the same thing in the same way? We answer for code, extending a recently introduced concept-circuit extraction method to a 2x2 design -- Python and Rust crossed with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B -- and measuring a complete inventory of grammatical concepts (58 Python, 57 Rust) identically in all four cells: the smallest design that separates what depends on the task, the language, and the model.
  The answer splits into three parts. What earns dedicated circuitry is set by the task: the models agree on which concepts receive circuits (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.638 for Python, 0.673 for Rust, both p < $10^{-7}$). Where those circuits sit is set by the model: Qwen processes concepts in a late band (~L17-19), DeepSeek at L6-7, for both languages. How circuits grow across layers is also set by the model: Qwen gives its atomic concepts an early spike that DeepSeek does not. "Are circuits universal?" thus has no single answer: yes for What, no for Where and How -- universality is a property of representational content, not of computational organisation.
  None of this structure was fixed in advance. The agreement could have landed anywhere between independence and identity; it lands at $ρ\approx 0.65$. Rust constructs receive 2-3x more concept-specific circuitry than their Python equivalents, in both models. Both models share neurons between the languages (6/7 and 7/7 paired constructs), DeepSeek 1.94x more than Qwen -- a direction no prior result predicts. And Qwen binds nine keywords of Rust's type-and-trait machinery into one tight neuron cluster (Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p < 0.001), a semantic dimension invisible in surface syntax. Ablation and linear probes confirm the circuits are functional.
  All claims are scoped to this 2x2; whether the per-model profile predicts a third model is the designed next test.

</details>


### [168] [Artificial Epanorthosis: Why large language models overuse a classical rhetorical figure, and how to mitigate it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21498)
*Federico Boggia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型中系统性出现的修辞现象“自我纠正”（epanorthosis），指出其过度使用源于训练数据中的宣传性文本和偏好调优（RLHF），并提出通过“自我纠正指数”按文体校准该现象，避免人类写作被机器风格同化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到大语言模型在生成文本时频繁使用‘自我纠正’这一修辞手法，与人类写作风格产生偏离，引发对模型输出风格过度模式化的担忧。

Method: 基于Fontanier的修辞分类体系，构建‘自我纠正指数’，衡量不同文体下模型与人类在使用该修辞上的密度差异，并在三种规模的指令调优模型上进行测试；结合LoRA适配器、指令调整和监督微调进行缓解实验。

Result: 发现模型在演讲类文本中过度使用该修辞（高达人类两到三倍，尤以意大利语为甚，且随模型规模增大而加剧），在非正式问答中则使用不足，而在议论文、新闻和百科类文本中接近人类水平；实验证明可通过简单指令减少约50%-75%的使用，监督微调几乎可完全消除，并能通过调节系数精确校准至人类水平。

Conclusion: 应追求模型在不同文体中对该修辞的使用与人类基准相校准，而非彻底消除；真正的风险在于人类可能反过来模仿机器的表达方式，导致写作风格的异化。

Abstract: A rhetorical figure that Cicero and Quintilian catalogued two thousand years ago reappears, systematically, in the text of large language models: epanorthosis, the self-correction of the specimen «This is not a course. It is a journey of transformation». This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. Building on evidence that models diverge from human rhetorical style, and on Fontanier's classification of epanorthosis as a figure of thought, it sets out a programme that scores the figure against genre-specific human baselines through an Epanorthosis Index (density relative to the human rate). A first measurement, on three sizes of one instruction-tuned model family, finds mis-calibration by register in both directions: the models overshoot in oratory (about twofold, near threefold in Italian, concentrated in the larger tiers) and undershoot in informal question-and-answer writing, while matching humans in argument, journalism, and encyclopedic prose. Three constructive contributions follow: a survey of mitigation techniques centred on lightweight LoRA adapters; a demonstration, in Italian, that a one-line instruction cuts the figure by half to nearly three-quarters and that a supervised-fine-tuning adapter removes it almost entirely, with a scaling coefficient that dials the reduction back onto the human rate; and the argument that the target is calibration to the human rate for each genre, not elimination. It closes on the stakes: the real risk is that we begin to write like the machines.

</details>


### [169] [DONDO: Open w2v-BERT Speech-Recognition Base Models for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21540)
*Paul Azunre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DONDO是一个基于w2v-BERT 2.0的开源自动语音识别模型家族，支持非洲多种语言，采用多阶段微调和轻量级语言条件机制，在保持单 checkpoint 的同时实现接近单语模型性能的多语言ASR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲许多语言缺乏带标注的语音数据，且现有ASR模型覆盖不足，因此需要构建开放、可自由使用的多语言语音识别模型以支持这些低资源语言。

Method: 基于w2v-BERT 2.0语音编码器，利用宗教文本中的朗读语音数据进行微调；提出两步（或三步）学习率退火微调策略，并引入一种轻量级语言条件机制，通过在声学特征前添加独热语言标识前缀帧来实现多语言模型的语言引导。

Result: 构建了21个单语和5个多语模型，覆盖27种非洲语言变体；多语言模型在五个语系中平均词错误率（WER）为10-13%，接近甚至超过单语基线；所有模型以Apache-2.0许可发布，支持自由商用。

Conclusion: DONDO通过高效微调策略和简洁的语言控制机制，实现了高性能、易扩展的非洲语言ASR系统，显著降低了多语言模型部署成本，同时推动了低资源语言技术的发展。

Abstract: We present DONDO, a family of open, permissively licensed automatic speech recognition (ASR) base models for African languages, built on the w2v-BERT 2.0 self-supervised speech encoder. DONDO comprises twenty-one monolingual models and five multilingual models spanning twenty-seven language varieties across Ghana, Sierra Leone, Nigeria, Senegal, Kenya and Zimbabwe. Models are fine-tuned primarily on read speech drawn from religious texts, which offer broad, license-clear and orthographically consistent coverage for languages that otherwise lack transcribed audio. We describe a two-step (and, for one family, three-step) learning-rate-annealed fine-tuning procedure that first adapts a shared multilingual model at a high learning rate and then anneals it to recover, and in several cases surpass, strong monolingual baselines. We further describe a lightweight language-conditioning mechanism that injects a one-hot language identity as a sequence of prefix frames prepended to the acoustic features, allowing a single multilingual checkpoint to be steered to a target language at inference. Across the five multilingual families the annealed models reach average word error rates (WER) of 10-13%, closing most of the gap to monolingual models while covering many languages in a single checkpoint. All models are released on the Hugging Face KhayaAI organisation under the Apache-2.0 license (attribution only) so that others may fine-tune them freely, including for commercial use. We provide a conservative estimate that the languages covered are spoken by on the order of one hundred million first-language speakers, and by substantially more when second-language use is included.

</details>


### [170] [MedGame: Storytelling Gamification Empowered by Large Language Models for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21570)
*Qian Wu,Xinrong Zhou,Zizhan Ma,Kai Chen,Zheyao Gao,Xun Lin,Hongqiu Wu,Longfei Gou,Yixiao Liu,Ann Sin Nga Lau,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedGame 是一个将静态临床案例转化为结构化、可执行的叙事游戏的框架，旨在通过决策中心的学习路径提升医学教育体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型在医学教育中的应用多集中于局部互动，如问答或单轮反馈，缺乏对整个临床案例进行系统性组织的能力。因此，需要一种能够提供全面、互动式学习体验的新方法。

Method: 提出 MedGame 框架，采用双引擎设计：医学叙事设计师生成基于案例的临床故事情节，包含状态和决策节点；故事导演则将其转换为依赖感知的多模态编排计划，并通过交互平台呈现。同时构建了包含5000个案例的 MedGame Bench 作为评估基准。

Result: 实验表明，针对任务的微调显著提升了开源大模型在 MedGame Bench 上的表现，缩小了与商业模型的差距。初步的学生研究表明，相比纯文本方式，学习者认为 MedGame 更具吸引力和实用性。

Conclusion: MedGame 为医学教育提供了一种新颖的、以决策为中心的互动学习方式，具有较高的应用潜力和教学价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for medical education, but most existing systems focus on localized interactions such as question answering or single-turn feedback, rather than organizing an entire clinical case into a decision-centered learning trajectory. We introduce \textit{MedGame}, a framework that transforms static clinical cases into structured, executable storytelling games. MedGame uses a dual-engine design: a Medical Narrative Designer synthesizes case-grounded clinical storylines with states and decision nodes, while a Story Director converts them into dependency-aware multimodal orchestration plans rendered by our released interactive platform. We construct MedGame Bench, a 5,000-case benchmark and evaluation protocol for Medical Narrative Generation and Story Direction. Experiments show that task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves open-source LLMs on MedGame Bench and narrows the gap with commercial models. A pilot student study further shows that learners perceive MedGame as more engaging and useful than text-only alternatives.

</details>


### [171] [Surprisal Theory is Tautological (without Rational Grounding)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21574)
*Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 惊讶理论认为语言单位在上下文中的处理难度是其在某种语言模型下的惊讶度的仿射函数，但本文指出该理论在缺乏约束条件下实为一种同义反复，必须通过理性主义干预才能打破这一循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示惊讶理论在当前形式下的逻辑缺陷，即其缺乏可证伪性，并探讨如何通过引入独立于经验数据的语言模型来解决这一问题。

Method: 通过数学论证表明，在温和的技术条件下，任何非负的处理难度度量都存在一个对应的语言模型使其惊讶度成为该难度的仿射函数；并回顾相关实证研究以挑战传统假设。

Result: 证明惊讶理论若不限定语言模型则无法做出可证伪预测；传统假设（即语言模型来自语料库分布）已被最新实证研究动摇；更好的语料拟合并不一定带来更优的心理预测。

Conclusion: 打破同义反复需要理性主义路径：相关语言模型应基于理解者的非经验性模型（如记忆限制或处理目标），而非依赖行为数据本身。

Abstract: Surprisal theory holds that the human processing difficulty of a linguistic unit in context is an affine function of its surprisal under some language model. I argue this claim is a tautology without further constraint: for any non-negative difficulty measure over units in context, there exists a language model whose surprisal is an affine function of it under mild technical conditions. Therefore, because any pattern of difficulty is consistent with some language model, without an additional constraint on the language model, surprisal theory makes no falsifiable predictions. The tautology was long obscured by an assumption implicit in two decades of psycholinguistic work---that the relevant language model is the distribution that generated the training corpus, so that improving corpus fit improves predictions of human behavior. Recent empirical work has undermined this assumption, demonstrating that better corpus models can be worse predictors of processing difficulty. I conclude that breaking the tautology requires a rationalist intervention, i.e., the relevant language model must be derived from a non-empirically motivated model of the comprehender, which could be based on, for instance, memory constraints or processing goals, and that, thus, does not depend on the behavioral data surprisal theory is meant to explain.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [HERMES: Heterogeneous Edge-Relational Multi-Head Embedded SSM Attention for Traffic Conflict Prediction at Signalized Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20505)
*Md Monzurul Islam,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出HERMES模型，通过异质图神经网络和安全度量感知的注意力机制，实现对城市交叉口交通冲突的精准场景级评估，显著优于现有方法，并展现出良好的跨场景泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通冲突评估方法多局限于成对交互或扁平化特征表示，难以捕捉多智能体间异质交互结构及动态演化的场景级风险，亟需更精细的建模方法。

Method: 将交通冲突评估建模为时序异质场景图分类任务，构建包含车辆与行人的异质节点及多类交互边的图结构，采用SSM增强的多头注意力机制、动态更新机制、安全感知池化与序列学习模块进行联合优化。

Result: 在10.9万条轨迹序列上验证，HERMES取得0.9898的AUC-ROC与0.8449的F1分数，在5%误报率下检出95.7%冲突；零样本外部测试AUC-ROC达0.9752，且支持少样本迁移提升目标站点性能。

Conclusion: 保留异质交互拓扑、安全语义边信息以及时序演化特征可显著提升场景级冲突分类性能，为信号交叉口的可迁移路侧安全监控提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Surrogate safety measures (SSMs) enable proactive traffic safety assessment, but many existing methods evaluate pairwise interactions independently or flatten multi-agent scenes into fixed feature vectors, limiting their ability to represent heterogeneous interaction structure and evolving scene-level risk. This study formulates traffic conflict assessment as temporal heterogeneous scene-graph classification and proposes HERMES, a heterogeneous edge-relational graph neural network with SSM-informed multi-head attention. Vehicles and pedestrians are represented as heterogeneous nodes, while vehicle-vehicle, vehicle-pedestrian, and pedestrian-pedestrian interactions are encoded as relation-specific edges with continuous kinematic and surrogate-safety descriptors. Relation-specific attention, dynamic node-edge updates, safety-aware graph pooling, and temporal sequence learning are jointly used to estimate scene-level conflict probability. HERMES was evaluated using 109,028 trajectory-derived sequences from a signalized urban intersection and tested on an independently collected comparable intersection dataset. Enhanced HERMES achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.9898 +/- 0.0013, an AUC-PR of 0.9412 +/- 0.0067, and an F1 score of 0.8449 +/- 0.0103. At a 5% false-alarm rate, it detected 95.7% of conflict sequences, outperforming the strongest Transformer baseline and XGBoost. In zero-shot external evaluation, HERMES achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.9752 and an AUC-PR of 0.7829. Joint source-target training further improved target-site performance with limited target-site data. These findings show that preserving heterogeneous interaction topology, safety-informed edge semantics, and short-term temporal evolution improves scene-level conflict classification and supports transferable roadside safety monitoring at signalized intersections.

</details>


### [173] [PhysCoRe: Physics-Corrected Residual World Models for Material-Aware Deformable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20653)
*Haocheng Yin,Shuohan Tao,Yongsheng Chen,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysCoRe，一种结合可微分MPM模拟器与两个前馈神经网络的物理校正残差世界模型，用于提升可变形物体在机器人操作下的演化预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖逐对象优化材料参数，速度慢且难以泛化，而端到端学习方法泛化能力差且常违反基本物理规律。

Method: 将不同的Material Point Method（MPM）模拟器与两个前馈神经网络结合：Material from Motion（MfM）模块从视觉观测中推断每个粒子的弹性，Residual from Dynamics（RfD）模块学习并预测模拟器动力学的残差修正。支持在线材料识别和基于不确定性的主动探索。

Result: 在真实可变形物体操作序列上的实验表明，PhysCoRe在预测准确性上优于现有最先进基线，且其预测置信度在物体几何上形成可靠分布。

Conclusion: PhysCoRe通过融合物理模拟与神经网络残差修正，实现了高精度、可解释且支持主动探索的可变形物体动态建模，具备良好的泛化性与实用性。

Abstract: Predicting how deformable objects evolve under robotic manipulation is a longstanding challenge. Existing approaches typically rely on per-object optimization to fit material parameters, which can be slow and cannot generalize, while end-to-end learned alternatives extrapolate poorly and often violate basic physical structure. We present PhysCoRe, a physics-corrected residual world model that couples a differentiable Material Point Method (MPM) simulator with two feed-forward neural networks. A material refinement module, Material from Motion (MfM), infers per-particle elasticity from visual observations, grounding the simulator in object-specific physics. A residual correction module, Residual from Dynamics (RfD), learns the discrepancy and predicts corrections to the simulator's internal dynamics, absorbing systematic biases that the analytical model cannot capture. This design also supports online material identification on novel objects. MfM adapts from limited interactions, and its predictive uncertainty steers further exploration toward the regions where its estimate is least confident. Experiments on real deformable-object manipulation sequences show that PhysCoRe outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in prediction accuracy, and that its predicted confidence forms a reliable distribution across the object's geometry, providing a natural signal for future confidence-guided exploration.

</details>


### [174] [Scalable Low-Cost Laboratory Automation: A Digital Twin-Integrated Robotic Platform for Autonomous Liquid Handling (RAINBOTTM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20662)
*Mohamed Rami Ayeche,Souhil Sid,Ahyen Mostofa,Rehaan Hussain,Ali Shayesteh,Fadwa El Mellouhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为RAINBOT的低成本、开源液体处理机器人，通过改装消费级3D打印机实现，并结合数字孪生和逆向设计框架，实现了可远程监控和人机协同的实验室自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业液体处理系统成本高、封闭性强且远程监管能力有限，限制了实验室自动化的普及。因此需要一种低成本、开放且可远程操作的替代方案。

Method: 将Elegoo Neptune 4 Max 3D打印机改装为液体处理机器人，替换挤出机为精密单通道移液器，使用线性执行器控制活塞和吸头弹射，通过G代码控制XYZ轴运动；开发基于浏览器的数字孪生系统，实现实时双向同步与远程监控；集成颜色传感器并连接CEID逆向设计框架以实现闭环实验。

Result: 成功完成了不同颜色水溶液的顺序交换实验，颜色混合结果与预期一致（平均绝对误差2%），验证了系统的准确性和实时追踪能力；整机成本低于1300美元，约为商用系统十分之一。

Conclusion: RAINBOT提供了一个低成本、开放可复现、具备远程监管和人机协同能力的自动化实验平台，推动自驱动实验室技术的普及。

Abstract: Laboratory automation accelerates discovery, yet its adoption is constrained by the high cost, proprietary design, and limited remote supervisability of commercial liquid-handling systems. This work presents RAINBOT\textsuperscript{TM}, a low-cost, openly reproducible liquid-handling robot built by converting a consumer-grade Cartesian 3D printer (Elegoo Neptune 4 Max). The printer extruder is replaced by a precision single-channel pipette actuated through the printer's own G-code-driven X--Y--Z gantry, with plunger and tip-eject motions effected by two compact linear actuators under Python control. To make experiments transparent and remotely supervisable, a browser-based digital twin is implemented to synchronise bidirectionally with the physical platform, mirroring kinematics and pipetting states in real time and exposing remote monitoring, intervention, and an emergency stop from any web browser. As a proof of concept, RAINBOT\textsuperscript{TM} performed sequential exchanges of differently coloured aqueous solutions while an integrated colour sensor quantified the resulting mixtures; measured red, yellow, and blue (RYB) responses agreed with expected mixing behaviour to within a mean absolute error of two percentage points, validating correct execution and real-time tracking. Closing the loop, the platform is coupled to the CEID\textsuperscript{TM} (Cooperative Explorer for Inverse Design) framework, which recasts experimentation from iterative manual guessing into a goal-directed inverse-design search while keeping a human in the loop. The complete hardware costs under US\$1300, which is roughly an order of magnitude below entry-level commercial handlers, thereby establishing an accessible physical--virtual framework for self-driving laboratory automation.

</details>


### [175] [Safe and Scalable Multi-Drone Payload Transport via CBF-based Reinforcement Learning with Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20665)
*Jaeyoun Choi,Oswin So,Songyuan Zhang,Cooper Taylor,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习的框架，用于安全且可扩展的多无人机协同载荷运输，通过2D抽象和分布式策略实现了在动态环境中的零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机载荷运输在实际应用中面临复杂耦合动力学、安全性与可扩展性不足的问题，尤其在动态非结构化环境中表现受限。

Method: 引入一种保留任务相关耦合特性的最小2D抽象模型，采用域随机化并结合离散图控制屏障函数的近端策略优化（DGPPO）算法训练完全分布式的控制策略。

Result: 实验证明单一学习策略可在不同团队规模和任务场景中实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，并在多组硬件实验中安全应对动态障碍物环境。

Conclusion: 所提框架实现了高效、安全、可扩展的多无人机载荷运输，具备强泛化能力，适用于复杂真实世界条件。

Abstract: Multi-drone payload transportation has emerged as a promising research paradigm with potential applications in construction, logistics, and disaster response. However, the complex coupled dynamics among drones, cables, and payloads pose significant challenges, and existing approaches remain limited in safety and scalability, particularly in dynamic and unstructured environments. In this work, we propose a learning-based framework for safe and scalable multi-drone cooperative payload transport. We introduce a minimal 2D abstraction that preserves the task-relevant drone-payload coupling required for coordination and safety, while remaining computationally efficient for large-scale learning. Using domain randomization over team size and physical parameters, we train a fully distributed policy via Discrete Graph Control Barrier Function Proximal Policy Optimization (DGPPO), enabling robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer without fine-tuning. Extensive real-world evaluations demonstrate that a single learned policy generalizes across varying team sizes and task scenarios. Furthermore, multi-group hardware experiments show that the same policy can safely operate in dynamic environments, where other drone teams act as moving obstacles. These results indicate that the proposed framework enables efficient, safe, and scalable multi-drone payload transportation with strong generalization to complex real-world conditions.

</details>


### [176] [Towards Capability-Aware Traversability Navigation for Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20679)
*Gianluca Capezzuto,Felipe Tommaselli,Matheus P. Angarola,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Capability-Aware Traversability (CAT)的框架，通过将机器人物理能力直接嵌入空间特征空间，实现对非结构化环境中可通行性的精准估计。该方法利用交互式标注流程生成基于物理轨迹的密集监督掩码，并结合SPADE模块调制机器人特定的可通行性向量，在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在跨形态平台间传递可通行性预测时，通常采用后期轨迹过滤，未能在学习表征中编码平台自身的物理约束。因此需要一种能够根据机器人本体特性动态调整可通行性判断的方法。

Method: 提出CAT框架，将物理极限嵌入空间特征空间；使用交互式标注流程生成基于物理轨迹的密集监督掩码；通过SPADE模块将机器人特定的可通行性向量调制到语义地形图中。

Result: 在人工标注和轨迹对齐的数据集上，CAT在基于排序的指标中表现最佳，相比最强基线在实际执行轨迹上的AUROC提升11.0%，在人类轨迹上的AUPRC提升15.8%；消融实验表明空间条件和每机器人原型能有效提升能力敏感性；在足式四足机器人和轮式滑移转向机器人上的部署实现了4.8Hz的实体感知避障。

Conclusion: CAT能够有效将机器人本体能力融入可通行性估计过程，提升了模型在不同平台间的适应性和安全性，验证了在嵌入式硬件上实现实时、具身感知导航的可行性。

Abstract: Estimating traversability in unstructured environments requires conditioning on robot embodiment, as the same terrain can be traversable for one platform and unsafe for another. Existing methods often transfer predictions across morphologies through late-stage trajectory filtering rather than encoding platform constraints in the learned representation. We propose Capability-Aware Traversability (CAT), a framework that embeds physical limits directly into the spatial feature space. CAT grounds dense supervision masks in physical trajectories through an interactive annotation pipeline and modulates semantic terrain maps with robot-specific traversability vectors through Spatially-Adaptive Denormalization (SPADE) blocks. Across human-annotated and trajectory-aligned datasets, CAT leads all ranking-based metrics, improving AUROC by 11.0% on physically executed trajectories and AUPRC by 15.8% on human traces over the strongest baseline. Ablations show that spatial conditioning and per-robot prototypes produce capability sensitivity beyond generic path prediction. Deployments on a legged quadruped and a wheeled skid-steer demonstrate embodiment-aware obstacle avoidance on embedded hardware at 4.8 Hz.

</details>


### [177] [FELT: Generating Tactile Signals from Vision for Visuo-Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20683)
*Zinan Li,Yiyang Ling,Yuming Gu,Binghao Huang,Chenhao Liang,Sharfin Islam,Hisham Bedri,John Chirikjian,Yunzhu Li,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FELT的基于学习的框架，能够从RGB图像中合成每根手指的压力触觉图像，从而减少对触觉传感器数据收集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于触觉传感器脆弱、专用且难以标准化，触觉数据比视觉数据更稀缺，因此需要一种方法来在没有大量真实触觉数据的情况下实现有效的视觉-触觉操作策略学习。

Method: FELT采用一个大型冻结视觉编码器和一个轻量级查询解码器，通过单次前馈过程预测触觉信号，并通过独立分支分别解码左右触觉传感器面板，以符合双指触觉传感器的物理拓扑结构。

Result: 在四个接触密集的操作任务上的实验表明，无论是生成的触觉图像还是潜在触觉特征，都能比仅使用视觉的基线方法提高策略成功率，其中潜在特征无需在策略训练或部署期间使用真实触觉传感器。

Conclusion: FELT能够在仅有RGB输入的情况下生成有效的触觉表示，显著增强了在缺乏真实触觉数据时的 visuo-tactile 操作能力，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: The sense of touch is central to manipulation, especially when vision is occluded or ambiguous. Although combining vision and touch improves manipulation, learning robust visuo-tactile policies requires substantial tactile data. Such data remains scarcer than visual data, because tactile sensors are fragile, specialized, and hard to standardize. To address this, we present Feature-Extracted Latent Tactile (FELT), a learning-based framework that synthesizes per-finger pressure tactile images from RGB observations, reducing the need for tactile-equipped data collection. FELT uses a large frozen visual encoder and a lightweight query decoder to predict tactile signals in a single feed-forward pass. To respect the physical topology of dual-finger tactile sensors, FELT decodes the left and right tactile sensor panels through separate branches, capturing the asymmetric contact patterns during interactions such as wiping, insertion, and in-hand rotation. At inference time, FELT only requires RGB data, allowing us to augment existing vision-only data with tactile observations, either as generated tactile images or as latent tactile features. Experiments on four contact-rich manipulation tasks demonstrate that both generated tactile images and latent tactile features improve policy success over vision-only baselines, with latent feature requiring no real tactile sensor during policy training or deployment. Supplementary material is available on our anonymous website: https://felt-tactile.github.io/.

</details>


### [178] [Decentralized UAV Swarms for Ground Target Protection in GPS- and Communication-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20710)
*Dimitria Silveria,Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Tiago Nascimento,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在无通信和GPS环境下的自主无人机群防御系统，通过机载传感器实现目标跟踪与群体协同，并采用去中心化的包围策略有效应对敌方无人机威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反无人机方法依赖于无人机间通信和全球定位，在现代战争中这些资源可能不可用，因此需要开发不依赖这些资源的新型防御系统。

Method: 使用卡尔曼滤波器仅基于相对测量来估计未知目标的状态和无人机群的位置，开发了适应目标运动的去中心化群体包围技术。

Result: 该方法在真实机器人平台上进行了广泛验证，证明其在检测、包围和拦截敌方无人机方面是有效的。

Conclusion: 所提出的管道能够在无通信和GPS的环境中有效保护地面目标免受无人机攻击，展示了自主无人机群在复杂环境下的应用潜力。

Abstract: The presence of UAVs in military operations has recently increased, also increasing the demand for defense systems against UAV attacks. UAVs can also be used as countermeasures. Most available methods rely on UAV-to-UAV communication and global positioning. However, such resources may not be available in modern warfare scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a pipeline for ground-target protection against UAV attacks that employs autonomous swarms of UAVs. We assume a communication- and GPS-denied environment in which the UAVs use onboard sensors to track the target and coordinate as a swarm. We developed Kalman filters to estimate the states of unknown targets and the positions of UAVs in the swarm using only relative measurements. Also, our strategy is to encircle the target of interest to maximize coverage. To achieve that, we propose a decentralized swarm encirclement technique that adapts to the target's motion. Our approach was extensively validated using real robots, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting, encircling, and intercepting hostile UAVs.

</details>


### [179] [Self-Supervised Bio-Inspired Robotic Trajectory Planning with Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20743)
*Miroslav Krupa,Miroslav Cibula,Kristína Malinovská*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受神经科学启发的自监督学习框架用于轨迹规划，利用前向和逆向模型作为内部监督机制，在含障碍物环境中验证了方法的可行性，同时发现并提出了缓解规划器对学习信号过度利用问题的训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决传统采样-based规划器在高维复杂环境中的计算昂贵问题以及基于模型的学习方法存在的样本效率低和泛化能力差的问题，本文探索一种无需依赖大量探索或专家示范的自监督学习框架。

Method: 采用基于前向模型和逆向模型的自监督学习框架作为内部监督机制，通过神经网络实现轨迹规划，并设计额外的训练机制和缓解策略来抑制对学习信号的过度利用。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在含障碍环境中的可行性，同时揭示了规划器倾向于过度利用前向与逆向模型提供的学习信号的现象。

Conclusion: 所提出的自监督框架具有潜力，但需进一步优化训练策略以避免对内部监督信号的过度依赖，从而提升规划性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning is a fundamental problem in robotics, requiring the generation of collision-free and efficient trajectories in a potentially complex environment. While sampling-based planners remain the dominant approach, they are often computationally expensive, particularly in high-dimensional spaces and obstacle-rich environments. Methods based on model learning offer a promising alternative, enabling efficient planning through a bounded number of forward passes through a neural trajectory planner, but commonly suffer from low sample efficiency or limited generalisation due to their reliance on exploration or expert demonstrations. This follow-up work tests our neuro-inspired self-supervised learning framework for trajectory planning that leverages forward and inverse models as the internal supervisory mechanism in an environment that contains an obstacle. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of the approach while revealing a tendency of our planner to exploit the learning signal provided by the forward and inverse models. To address this issue, additional training regimes and mitigation strategies are proposed and evaluated.

</details>


### [180] [A real-time RGB-D perception pipeline for autonomous impact hammers in mining: self-filtering, rock segmentation and rock-breaking poses generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20748)
*Martín Gallegos,Francisco Leiva,Patricio Loncomilla,Michelle Cortés,Javier Ruiz-del-Solar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RGB-D的实时感知系统，用于实现矿用液压冲击锤的自动化操作，能够在嵌入式硬件上以约10 Hz运行，具备低延迟和闭环控制潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下采矿中冲击锤通常为远程操作，限制了作业效率，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 结合基于图像的实例分割与几何点云处理，同步生成可执行的破岩位姿和无机器人干扰的三维工作区表示。

Result: 在代表性缩尺场景中的实验表明，该系统可在约675 ms总延迟下以约10 Hz实时运行，适用于自主化冲击锤操作。

Conclusion: 所提出的感知管道为实现冲击锤的实时自主作业提供了可行方案，具有实际应用前景。

Abstract: Impact hammers, also known as rock-breakers, are essential machines in mining operations, where they perform secondary reduction. In underground mining, these machines are typically teleoperated, limiting operational efficiency. This paper presents a real-time RGB-D perception pipeline as a step towards automating the operation of hydraulic impact hammers used in mining. The proposed system simultaneously generates operationally feasible rock-breaking poses and a robot-free 3D representation of the workspace. The proposed approach combines image-based instance segmentation with geometric point cloud processing, and operates on embedded hardware at approximately 10 Hz with a total latency of around 675 ms, enabling responsive closed-loop behavior when integrated with a control system. Experimental results in a representative scaled scenario demonstrate that the proposed system is suitable for real-time autonomous impact hammer operation.

</details>


### [181] [Emergent Compositional Skills in Mixture-of-Experts VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20771)
*Shlok Shah,Rhiaan Jhaveri,Tharun Kumar Tiruppali Kalidoss,Chirayu Nimonkar,Ishaan Javali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何从专家演示中端到端地学习组合式机器人策略，提出使用简化的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）动作头的VLA模型，能够自发地将任务分解为可重用、可解释的低级行为模块，在保持与单体模型相当性能的同时，实现了专家模块的功能专门化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在没有预设任务分解或层次结构的前提下，通过数据驱动的方式让模型自主学会将复杂任务分解为可复用和可解释的子行为，从而提升机器人策略的模块化和可解释性。

Method: 采用具有简化MoE动作头的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过专家机制隐式学习任务分解：路由器负责高层任务序列规划，各个专家则对应不同的低层行为策略，并在多个任务间共享。

Result: 实验发现，学习到的专家模块在不同任务中被广泛复用，并且每个专家表现出语义上清晰区分的低级行为（如抓取、移动等），MoE模型在任务性能上与单体模型相当，但展现出显著的专家专业化特征。

Conclusion: MoE架构能够在无监督任务分解的情况下，从专家示范中自发涌现出模块化、可解释的机器人策略，是实现数据驱动的组合式策略学习的重要一步。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning compositional robot policies end-to-end from expert demonstrations, without any pre-specified notion of task decomposition or hierarchy. We ask whether a VLA trained with a simplified Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head can emergently learn to decompose tasks into reusable, interpretable primitives. We find that learned experts are heavily reused across tasks and consistently correspond to qualitatively distinct low-level behaviors, suggesting that the router implicitly learns to perform high-level sequencing while experts serve as compositional primitives. Our MoE matches the task performance of a monolithic baseline while demonstrating meaningful expert specialization, a step toward modular, interpretable robot policies that emerge from data alone.

</details>


### [182] [Socially Consistent Multi-Robot Navigation Using Decoupled Planning and Trajectory Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20772)
*Matthew M. Sato,Kincho H. Law*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分去中心化的多机器人导航框架，通过将全局路径规划与轨迹协调解耦，实现可预测且符合社会规范的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短期人感知规划方法缺乏长期一致性，导致行为不可预测，影响人类对机器人的接受度。

Method: 提出改进的A*算法，在代价函数中嵌入宏观社会规范，并构建共享的社会路径图；利用受限路径结构建立混合整数凸优化模型进行轨迹协调。

Result: 实验表明该方法能生成符合社会规范的可预测路径，降低规划负担，提升多机器人系统在大规模场景下的协调效率。

Conclusion: 在路径规划阶段引入社会一致性可有效简化多机器人协调问题，并提高导航的可预测性与社会合规性。

Abstract: The successful integration of mobile robots in human-centric environments requires navigation that is not only safe and efficient, but also predictable and aligned with social conventions, key precursors for human comfort and acceptance. While significant research addresses short-term human-aware planning, these methods often lack mechanisms for ensuring consistent and predictable behaviors across long horizons. Without socially aware long-term planners, local planners are overburdened, resulting in inefficient and locally reactive movements that undermine predictability. This paper introduces a partially decentralized framework that generates predictable and socially consistent multi-robot motion by decoupling global path planning from trajectory coordination. First, we propose a modified A* planner that embeds macroscopic social norms into the planner cost function. Planned paths are shared across mobile robots to collaboratively build a social graph of established routes, which enforces path consistency and reduces future planning effort. Second, we leverage the emergent structure of the socially constrained paths to formulate the multi-robot trajectory coordination problem as a mixed-integer convex program. The convex program enables efficient computation of conflict- free trajectories, scaling effectively to large fleets and supporting dynamic task assignment. Our results demonstrate that enforcing social consistency at the path planning stage produces predictable, socially compliant mobile robot paths and simplifies the otherwise complex problem of multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [183] [Robostral Navigate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20785)
*Arjun Majumdar,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Benjamin Tibi,Chris Bamford,Elliot Chane-Sane,Guillaume Lample,Khyathi Raghavi Chandu,Ludovic Ho Fuh,Mathieu Poiree,Olivier Duchenne,Rosalie Millner,Srijan Mishra,Theo Cachet,Thomas Chabal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robostral Navigate是一种基于8B参数的视觉-语言模型，仅使用单目RGB图像进行机器人导航，具备跨平台泛化能力、高效训练和卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统依赖深度传感器或多相机配置，限制了硬件兼容性并增加部署成本；需要一种可扩展、低感知假设、适用于多种机器人形态的解决方案。

Method: 提出Robostral Navigate，使用单目RGB图像输入，在图像空间内通过指向目标位置预测航点；采用前缀缓存训练策略将整个episode打包为单个序列，并结合树状注意力掩码防止对未来动作泄露，辅以强化学习提升探索与恢复能力。

Result: 在R2R-CE上达到77.4%成功率，超越最佳单目方法10.5点，优于最强多相机或深度系统5.3点；在RxR-CE上达到75.1%成功率，优于所有单目基线；训练效率提升22倍，训练时间从数月缩短至数天。

Conclusion: Robostral Navigate实现了高可扩展性的视觉导航，仅依赖单目摄像头即可在多种机器人平台上高效部署，同时在性能和训练效率方面显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Deploying navigation systems at scale requires a recipe that minimizes sensor assumptions, generalizes across robot embodiments, and trains efficiently. Yet, today's best systems depend on depth sensors, multi-camera rigs, or pre-built maps, limiting the hardware they support and increasing deployment cost. We introduce Robostral Navigate, an 8B vision-language model built around this scalability objective. The model consumes only a stream of monocular RGB images - the most ubiquitous sensor across robotic platforms and predicts waypoints by pointing to the next target location in the current camera view. Operating purely in image space, rather than robot-specific coordinates, makes the policy naturally robust to changes in camera intrinsics and scene scale, enabling deployment across wheeled, legged, and aerial robots without recalibration. We generate 2.4 million trajectories across 350k simulated scenes to reduce the reliance on real-world data collection and scale easily. We further introduce a prefix-caching training recipe that packs entire episodes into single training sequences, reducing training tokens by 22x and cutting training time from months to days. A tree-based attention mask prevents conditioning on previous ground-truth actions, encouraging visually grounded action prediction, and reinforcement learning is used to further improve exploration and recovery capabilities. On the Room-to-Room and Room-Across-Room in Continuous Environments (R2R-CE and RxR-CE) benchmarks, Robostral Navigate sets a new state of the art. On R2R-CE, it achieves a 77.4% success rate, surpassing the best monocular method by 10.5 points and the strongest depth- or multi-camera system by 5.3 points despite using only a single RGB camera. On RxR-CE, it reaches 75.1% success rate, outperforming all monocular baselines.

</details>


### [184] [URF: A Unified Robot Control-Policy Framework for Stable Contact Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20912)
*Jiyou Shin,Youngjin Seo,Jaeseog Won,Sungwon Seo,Hyunjun Kim,Seokmin Yoon,Tuan Luong,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的机器人控制-策略框架（URF），通过结合柔顺动作预测与阻抗-导纳控制，提升了在刚性接触任务中的操作稳定性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的学习型操作策略将动作预测与底层控制分离，在刚性接触中容易导致不稳定、跟踪误差或工具损坏，因此需要一种更紧密集成的控制-策略框架。

Method: URF框架基于多模态观测，预测虚拟目标、刚度矩阵和阻抗-导纳切换比；利用实测接触力构建切换比标签，监督控制器模式的预测；实现柔顺控制与动态行为调节的统一。

Result: 在翻盒和划线任务中，URF相比仅使用导纳控制的方法，显著提高了任务成功率，并减少了快速力积累、大力振荡、工具断裂和机器人安全停机等故障模式。

Conclusion: 接触感知策略不仅应预测柔顺动作，还应预测执行这些动作时的控制器行为，URF验证了这种联合预测的有效性与必要性。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies usually predict robot actions from sensory observations and leave their execution to a separate low-level controller. In rigid contact, this separation can be problematic: the same motion to a virtual target or compliant motion command can lead to unstable contact, tracking error, excessive loading, or tool damage, depending on the low-level controller. In this paper, we propose a \textit{Unified Robot Control-Policy Framework} (URF), which connects compliant action prediction with unified impedance-admittance control. Given multimodal observations, URF predicts a virtual target, a stiffness matrix, and an impedance-admittance switch ratio. The switch ratio determines when the controller should behave more like admittance control for accurate motion tracking and when it should move toward impedance control for safer rigid contact. Because demonstration data do not provide ground-truth environment stiffness, we construct switch-ratio labels from measured contact forces and use them to supervise controller-mode prediction. Across box-flipping and line-pressing tasks, URF achieves higher task success rates while reducing failure modes observed with admittance-only execution, including rapid force buildup, large force oscillations, tool breakage, and robot safety stops. These results suggest that contact-aware policies benefit from predicting not only compliant actions but also the controller behavior used to execute them. Project page: https://jiyou384.github.io/urf_project_page/

</details>


### [185] [Deep Reinforcement-Learning-Guided Model Predictive Control for Preventing Overtakes in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20973)
*Yufei Xi,Yijie Liao,Tulga Ersal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分层强化学习引导的模型预测控制框架，用于自动驾驶赛车中的防御性阻挡问题，能够在接近车辆动力极限的情况下有效延缓更快对手的超车行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶赛车中，传统的轨迹优化方法主要关注圈速最小化，而缺乏对对抗性场景（如防御超车）的有效策略。本文旨在解决如何让较慢车辆在动态极限下通过空间占位策略有效阻挡更快对手的问题。

Method: 采用分层架构：上层使用Soft Actor-Critic强化学习算法在Frenet坐标系下生成具有几何感知的防御参考路径；下层将该参考路径作为空间正则项嵌入非线性模型预测控制中，并考虑摩擦约束进行实时轨迹优化。

Result: 在Thunderhill West赛道的仿真中，平均超车时间从8.8秒增加到14.6秒，显著降低了对手的前进进度；车辆利用了83.4%的可用轮胎力，平均求解时间为33.3毫秒（标准差13.9毫秒），满足高速实时交互需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能有效实现近极限条件下的防御性阻挡，结合了强化学习的策略生成能力与模型预测控制的精确跟踪性能，具备应用于实时高动态对抗场景的潜力。

Abstract: This paper addresses defensive blocking in autonomous racing, where a vehicle must prevent a faster opponent from overtaking while operating near its dynamic limits. Different from lap-time minimization, we formulate defense as a spatial occupancy regulation problem via a hierarchical reinforcement-learning guided model predictive control framework. A Soft Actor-Critic strategic layer operates in the Frenet domain to generate geometry-aware defensive references, which are embedded into the nonlinear model predictive control formulation as spatial regularization under friction constraints. Evaluated on the Thunderhill West circuit in simulation, the framework increases average overtake time from 8.8 s to 14.6 s while significantly reducing opponent progress. Meanwhile, it allows the vehicle to utilize 83.4% of available tire force. The framework achieves a 33.3 ms mean solve time (13.9 ms std), supporting real-time high-speed adversarial interaction.

</details>


### [186] [Distributed Model-Based Diffusion For Scalable Multi-Robot Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20992)
*Haejoon Lee,Xinyi Wang,Taekyung Kim,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式模型扩散（DMBD）方法，用于解决多机器人系统在复杂环境中轨迹优化的可扩展性与效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的扩散方法在多机器人系统中存在维度灾难和需要全局信息的问题，导致采样效率低且难以扩展。

Method: 设计了一种分布式的服务器-机器人框架，将反向扩散过程分解为局部条件反向扩散过程，各机器人在自身控制空间内独立去噪，并基于服务器聚合的其他机器人轨迹估计进行条件更新。

Result: 在多种场景（如目标交换、多层覆盖、停车、高峰期交通）的仿真中，DMBD能在亚秒级时间内解决复杂的协调任务，表现出强可扩展性并显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DMBD通过分布式架构有效克服了高维推理和全局依赖的瓶颈，为多机器人轨迹优化提供了一种高效、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization for multi-robot systems remains a critical challenge, particularly when navigating highly non-convex, non-linear, and non-differentiable environments. While Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) has recently emerged as a promising sampling-based optimization paradigm for single-robot trajectory generation, extending it to multi-robot systems results in a centralized, high-dimensional inference problem that (i) suffers from poor sample efficiency due to the curse of dimensionality and (ii) requires global access to all robots' dynamics, constraints, and objectives.
