To address this, we propose Distributed Model-Based Diffusion (DMBD), a distributed server-robot framework that decomposes the reverse diffusion process into local conditional reverse diffusion processes. This decomposition enables each robot to iteratively perform denoising independently within its own control subspace while conditioning on the current trajectory estimates of the other robots that are aggregated and broadcast by the server. Extensive simulations in goal swapping, multi-floor coverage, parking, and rush-hour scenarios demonstrate that DMBD achieves strong scalability, solving many challenging coordination tasks in sub-seconds and significantly outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [187] [TableVerse: A Large-scale Tabletop Dataset with Real-world Grounded Layouts for Generalizable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21017)
*Boyuan Wang,Yue Zhang,Xutao Xue,Xueyu Song,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TableVerse提出了一种从真实世界图像中自动重建高保真、物理一致的桌面场景的Real2Sim管道，并构建了包含10万个多样化环境与操作轨迹的大规模数据集，为可泛化的机器人操作策略研究提供了高质量数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化合成方法生成的场景常存在物理不合理性和缺乏真实复杂杂乱布局的问题，限制了可泛化操作策略的发展，因此需要一种能从真实无结构图像中重建高保真且物理可行场景的新方法。

Method: 提出TableVerse框架，通过全自动Real2Sim流程将非结构化的网络图像转换为具有精确尺度、真实拓扑和机械稳定性的仿真就绪桌面环境，并集成任务条件下的轨迹生成模块以合成无碰撞的操作演示。

Result: 成功构建了TableVerse-100K数据集，包含10万个独特的物理一致场景及其对应的高质量拾取放置轨迹，展现出多样资产组合、真实空间分布和高保真特性。

Conclusion: TableVerse为可泛化机器人操作提供了高度可扩展且高保真的数据基础，推动了基于真实世界复杂性进行策略训练的研究方向。

Abstract: The development of generalizable robotic manipulation policies is inherently bounded by the availability of large-scale, high-fidelity scene data. While recent automated synthesis methods attempt to bridge this gap via text-to-layout hallucination or simplified procedural generation, they frequently suffer from physical implausibility and fail to capture the complex, dense clutter of actual human environments. In this paper, we introduce TableVerse, a fully automated Real2Sim pipeline that shifts the paradigm from imaginative layout generation to deterministic reconstruction from unstructured, in-the-wild image data. Our framework seamlessly processes unscripted internet media into high-fidelity, simulation-ready tabletop environments with accurate metric scales, authentic topologies, and verified mechanical stability. Furthermore, an automated task-conditioned trajectory generation framework is integrated to synthesize high-quality, collision-free pick-and-place demonstrations. Leveraging this complete pipeline, we construct the TableVerse-100K Dataset, a large-scale corpus comprising 100,000 unique, physically consistent environments paired with interactive manipulation trajectories. By capturing diverse asset compositions, realistic spatial distributions, and high-quality demonstrations, TableVerse-100K establishes a highly scalable and high-fidelity data foundation, providing significant value to facilitate future research in generalizable robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [188] [ZONDA: Zero-shot Object Navigation with Dynamic Avoidance in Multi-floor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21025)
*Shaomin Liang,Xuanhong Liao,Shiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ZONDA的零样本目标导航框架，能够在多楼层、动态环境中实现鲁棒的物体导航，并集成启发式多楼层规划、多视角目标验证和动态行人避让模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法局限于静态单层环境，难以应对真实世界中的多楼层结构和动态行人干扰，限制了实际应用。

Method: ZONDA包含三个核心组件：基于高度差可通行地图的启发式多楼层路径规划、利用视觉-语言模型进行多尺度观测交叉验证的目标确认、以及对移动行人的跟踪与运动预测以实现动态避障。

Result: 在真实双足机器人TITA及HM3D、MP3D的大规模仿真中验证了ZONDA的有效性，在动态基准HM3D-DYNA上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ZONDA实现了无需专门训练即可跨楼层导航并避开动态障碍物的能力，提升了物体导航系统在复杂真实环境中的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In Object Goal Navigation task, existing methods are typically restricted to static and single-floor environments, ignoring cross-floor topologies and dynamic pedestrian, which limits their real-world deployment. To address these limitations, we propose ZONDA, a zero-shot object navigation with dynamic avoidance framework. In particular, ZONDA integrates three core components: (i) Heuristic multi-floor planning: from height-difference traversable maps, enables stair traversal and cross-floor exploration without a platform-specific learned controller; (ii) Multi-view target verification: cross-checks multi-scale observations with a vision-language model, significantly reducing false positives; and (iii) Dynamic pedestrian avoidance: explicitly tracks and predicts moving pedestrians to generate anticipatory behaviors. Evaluated on a real Direct Drive Tech TITA biped robot and extensive simulations on HM3D and MP3D, ZONDA achieves significantly improved results. Moreover, ZONDA can maintain robust navigation on the dynamic benchmark HM3D-DYNA compared to the existing baseline.

</details>


### [189] [A Real-Time Generalized Nash Equilibrium Framework for Interaction-Aware Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21043)
*Nouhed Naidja,Mohamed-Cherif Rahal,Steve Pechberti,Stéphane Font,Guillaume Sandou,Marc Revilloud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP）的自动驾驶决策框架，通过粒子群优化算法实现实时求解，并在真实混合交通场景中验证了其安全性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在混合交通环境中需应对人类驾驶员行为的不确定性，传统解耦优化方法难以有效建模交互关系，因此需要一种能显式处理共享约束的决策框架。

Method: 将驾驶交互建模为广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP），引入共享的安全与几何约束，并采用基于粒子群优化（PSO）的专用求解器进行实时求解。

Result: 在真实测试轨道上使用雷诺Zoé自动驾驶车与人类驾驶员互动实验表明，该系统能生成舒适、类人的轨迹，且求解器可在50毫秒内收敛。

Conclusion: 所提出的GNEP框架能够有效捕捉自动驾驶车与人类驾驶员之间的动态交互，兼顾安全性与实时性，适用于复杂混合交通环境中的决策制定。

Abstract: Safe and efficient navigation in mixed-traffic environments remains a critical challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs), primarily due to the complex interdependence between the AV's decisions and the unpredictable reactions of human drivers. This paper introduces a comprehensive decision-making framework that formulates the driving interaction as a Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problem (GNEP). Unlike decoupled optimization approaches, this framework explicitly models shared safety and geometric constraints, ensuring that the feasibility of the AV's strategy is dynamically linked to the opponent's actions. To solve this non-convex problem in real-time, we propose a dedicated solver based on Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO). The complete architecture was validated on a test track using a real autonomous Renault Zoé interacting with a human driver. Experimental results demonstrate the system's ability to handle critical scenarios by generating comfortable, human-like trajectories. Benchmarks confirm the solver's operational feasibility, achieving convergence in under 50 ms.

</details>


### [190] [GuidedAttention: Interpretable and Correctable Visual Attention for OOD-Robust Robot Manipulation via Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21049)
*Masaki Murooka,Ryoichi Nakajo,Keisuke Shirai,Tomohiro Motoda,Hanbit Oh,Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GuidedAttention的视觉运动模仿学习框架，通过引入可解释和可纠正的视觉注意力作为中间表示来提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端的视觉运动策略难以让人类理解或纠正模型的视觉注意力，缺乏可解释性和交互性。

Method: 从图像中预测任务相关的注意力关键点，并以此条件化基于扩散模型的动作策略；用户可在执行初始化时检查并修正关键点，跟踪模块会在后续执行中传播修正后的注意力。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该方法在位置和外观分布外（OOD）条件下 consistently 提升了机器人操作性能。

Conclusion: GuidedAttention通过显式的可解释注意力机制增强了人机协作的可控性与鲁棒性，尤其在OOD场景下表现更优。

Abstract: End-to-end visuomotor policies provide little opportunity for humans to understand or correct the policy's visual attention. We propose GuidedAttention, a visuomotor imitation learning framework that introduces interpretable and correctable visual attention as an explicit intermediate representation. Task-relevant attention keypoints are predicted from camera images and condition a diffusion-based action policy. Users can inspect and optionally correct selected keypoints once at rollout initialization, after which the corrected attention is automatically propagated throughout execution by a tracking module. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that GuidedAttention consistently improves robot manipulation performance, particularly under positional and appearance out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions.

</details>


### [191] [Human-Inspired Framework for Robotic Craniotomy: Integrating Multimodal Fusion and Adaptive Trajectory Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21058)
*Renzhen Le,Xiao Zhang,Di Wu,Yuanyu Wei,Jiachen Zhu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种类人闭环机器人颅骨切开框架，结合术前规划与术中执行，实现安全自主的颅骨手术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开环机器人系统依赖术前图像，无法补偿术中配准误差和组织变形，存在安全隐患。

Method: 采用自适应双轮廓融合算法生成轨迹，结合力和声学信号的多模态网络（CMA-TCN-Transformer）与自适应贝叶斯滤波进行突破检测，并通过原位投影轨迹调整策略动态补偿深度偏差。

Result: 牛肋骨实验显示97%的突破预测准确率，检测延迟0.048±0.097秒，最大超调0.29毫米；4例离体颅骨实验均无硬膜损伤。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭环控制系统可有效提升机器人颅骨切开术的安全性与自主性。

Abstract: Manual craniotomy is a high-risk, skill-dependent procedure associated with surgeon fatigue and potential dural injury. While robotic approaches have improved safety, existing open-loop systems rely solely on preoperative images and cannot compensate for intraoperative registration errors or tissue deformation. To address this, we propose a human-inspired closed-loop robotic craniotomy framework that intelligently integrates preoperative planning with intraoperative execution. An adaptive dual-contour fusion algorithm is employed to generate trajectories that conform to complex cranial geometries while maintaining a consistent tool-bone relative pose. For intraoperative perception, a multimodal two-stage cross-modal attention block (CMA)-temporal convolutional network (TCN)-Transformer network combined with an adaptive Bayesian filter fuses force and acoustic signals to achieve robust breakthrough detection under varying bone conditions. Upon detection, an in-situ projection-based trajectory adjustment strategy dynamically compensates for depth deviations, enabling safe residual bone isolation. Experiments on bovine ribs show a breakthrough prediction accuracy of 97%, a detection latency of 0.048 +/- 0.097 s, and a maximum overshoot of 0.29 mm. All four ex vivo cranial experiments were successfully completed without dural injury. These results demonstrate that the proposed cybernetic framework enables safe and autonomous craniotomy with highly effective closed-loop control.

</details>


### [192] [RL-MACRO: A Cybernetic Closed-Loop Intelligence Framework for Multimodal Adaptive Robotic Craniotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21113)
*Xiao Zhang,Jiaxuan Li,Renzhen Le,Di Wu,Chao Sun,Jiachen Zhu,Haoyuan Zhang,Xiang Li,Jian Liu,Zhenzhi Ying,Pengfei Zhang,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RL-MACRO的闭环智能框架，用于实现安全高效的自主骨切割，结合多模态感知、自适应决策与机器人执行，能够在部分可观环境下准确估计隐藏温度状态并动态优化手术参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主颅骨切削手术中由于组织特性未知且无法直接测量温度，导致机械过载和热损伤风险高，传统方法难以在保证效率的同时确保安全性，因此需要一种能够应对不确定环境的自适应闭环控制框架。

Method: 提出RL-MACRO框架：采用CNN-LSTM融合力和声音信号以重建隐藏温度状态；利用离线隐式Q学习（IQL）策略结合多传感器特征构建信念状态；设计双头Actor动态调节进给速率、主轴转速和切削深度；通过在线轨迹重规划和速度伺服实现运动控制。

Result: 在牛肋骨和六具山羊离体颅骨上的实验表明，该系统能准确估计温度（R²=0.939，MAE=1.717°C），具备强健的状态感知能力，可自适应恢复异常力/温升，并在不规则表面实现平滑切割。

Conclusion: RL-MACRO为自主骨切削提供了一个数据驱动的、类神经控制的解决方案，实现了在部分可观测环境下的安全、高效与鲁棒操作，推动了智能化手术机器人的发展。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic craniotomy requires continuous regulation of tool-tissue interactions to mitigate mechanical overload and thermal damage while maintaining surgical efficiency. However, this process is inherently partially observable due to unknown, time-varying tissue properties and the inability to directly measure cutting temperatures under physical occlusion. To address these challenges, we propose RL-MACRO, a cybernetic closed-loop intelligence framework that couples multimodal perception, adaptive decision-making, and robotic execution. This framework empowers the surgical robot to autonomously perceive inaccessible states from partial sensory feedback and dynamically optimize its behaviors under uncertain environment. A CNN-LSTM observer first fuses force and sound feedback to reconstruct the hidden temperature state (R^2=0.939, MAE = 1.717 deg C). This reconstructed temperature, alongside multi-sensor features, forms the belief state for an offline Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) policy. A novel dual-head Actor dynamically coordinates the feed rate, spindle speed, and cutting depth to optimize efficiency within strict safety bounds. These decisions are seamlessly translated into spatial motions via online trajectory re-planning and velocity servoing. Experiments on bovine ribs and six ex vivo goat skulls validate the system's robust perception, adaptive recovery from force/temperature excursions, and smooth execution on irregular surfaces, establishing a data-driven cybernetic paradigm for safe and efficient autonomous bone cutting.

</details>


### [193] [FORGE-plus: Force-Budgeted Recovery for Contact-Rich Assembly with a Frozen LLM Supervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21227)
*Kyupaeck Jeff Rah,Midum Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文本提示的两层强化学习框架，利用冻结的大语言模型（LLM）为不同物体分配力限制并选择恢复动作，结合底层控制器在严格力约束下实现无损装配。实验表明该方法在模拟环境中能有效完成高精度插入任务并从失败中恢复，且避免了物体破损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧间隙装配中，如何为不同物体设定安全的力限制并在不超限的情况下从插入失败中恢复，是力控强化学习实际部署的关键挑战。现有方法难以兼顾安全性与适应性，需要一种无需直接力控制即可智能决策的策略。

Method: 提出一个两层框架：上层使用冻结的纯文本大语言模型（LLM），根据物体类型指定力上限，并基于紧凑的文本力特征选择恢复动作；下层由低级控制器强制执行力限制，恢复策略不可增大力限。所有操作在刚体仿真中进行，破坏力阈值对策略隐藏。

Result: 单一策略在256次评估中全部成功，未发生破损，准确判断释放时机，平均峰值力为5.4 N；在注入夹持滑移故障下，力特征恢复策略分别解决了40%和64%的失败，而‘加大压力’基线无效或导致频繁破损；PPO算法在严格力约束下未能学会任务，学习式释放策略也未成功。

Conclusion: 冻结的文本大语言模型可有效用于力敏任务的高层决策，包括力限分配与基于文本签名的恢复动作选择，在不直接控制力的情况下提升系统安全性与鲁棒性。该框架适用于严格力约束下的精密操作，但当前仅验证于仿真环境。

Abstract: Force-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) enables tight-clearance assembly under a commanded force ceiling, but practical deployment requires determining an appropriate force limit for each object and recovering from insertion failures without exceeding it. We present a two-layer framework in which a frozen, text-only large language model (LLM) assigns a per-object force ceiling before execution and selects recovery maneuvers from a fixed action menu using compact textual force signatures. The LLM never controls force directly: a low-level controller enforces the force ceiling, the recovery policy cannot increase it, and the hidden breaking-force threshold is known only to the evaluator.
  We evaluate the framework on fragile bottle placement and 0.4 mm diametral-clearance gear insertion using two grippers (Robotiq 2F-140 and Franka Panda hand). A single policy passes 256/256 evaluation episodes on both fragile and robust objects without breakage, correctly predicts release timing, and completes a full table-pick-and-insert pipeline with a mean peak force of 5.4 N. Under injected in-grip slip, the force-signature recovery strategy resolves 40% and 64% of failures on the two grippers, whereas a press-harder baseline is either ineffective or causes frequent breakage. We also report negative results, including the failure of PPO to solve the task under strict force constraints and unsuccessful learned release strategies. All experiments are conducted in rigid-body simulation with hidden force-threshold breakage; no sim-to-real claim is made.

</details>


### [194] [Factorized Spatio-Temporal Convolutions for Human Pose Estimation from Planar Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21309)
*Simone Arreghini,Mirko Nava,Nicholas Carlotti,Antonio Paolillo,Alessandro Giusti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于时空块的轻量级网络，用于从全向平面LiDAR序列中实现人体检测与相对2D姿态估计，并通过跨模态自监督学习进行训练，在计算资源受限的服务机器人上实现实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人通常仅配备全向平面LiDAR和低功耗处理器，现有方法多依赖摄像头、3D LiDAR或GPU计算，难以部署。因此需要一种适用于低成本、低算力平台的人体定位与朝向估计方案。

Method: 设计基于Space-Time Blocks的轻量级网络，将沿扫描线的空间处理与跨扫描帧的时间聚合分离；利用窄视场RGB-D身体追踪器在传感器重叠区域提供跨模态自监督信号，实现无需人工标注的训练。

Result: 在距离误差上降低38%，位置误差降低28%，朝向误差降低15%；在FROG公开数据集上表现优异，支持CPU实时推理，并在真实服务机器人场景中验证有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法适合部署于计算资源受限的服务机器人，实现了高效、准确的周围人体感知与相对姿态估计，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Localizing nearby humans and estimating their facing direction are key capabilities for safe navigation and socially aware human-robot interaction. Many pose-estimation pipelines target cameras and 3D LiDAR or assume GPU-class compute, whereas service robots are often equipped only with omnidirectional planar LiDARs and modest onboard processors. We address omnidirectional human detection and relative 2D pose estimation from planar LiDAR sequences with a lightweight network based on Space-Time Blocks, which explicitly separate spatial processing along scan rays from temporal aggregation across scans. Our network processes 360° LiDAR sequences to output per-ray human presence, distance, and relative orientation. We train it via cross-modal self-supervision from a narrow RGB-D body tracker in the sensors' overlap region, removing the need for manual LiDAR labels. Quantitative experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms a parameter-matched baseline model, reducing errors in distance (-38%), position (-28%), and orientation (-15%). We further benchmark on the public FROG dataset, report real-time CPU inference on a service robot, and validate with in-field demonstrations, supporting its suitability for spatial perception on computationally constrained service robots.

</details>


### [195] [Grasp, Handover, Rotate: Bimanual Object Reorientation via Compositional Diffusion and Energy-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21341)
*Wun Lam Yeung,Wenjun Liu,Yui Cheung Yu,Zhengyan Lambo Qin,Qijin She,Heng Li,Ziqi Wang,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BiCompoDiff的组合式扩散与能量模型框架，用于双手机械臂物体重定向任务，能够联合优化抓取、传递、再抓取和运动规划，在多种约束下显著提升了成功率和轨迹平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在碰撞、运动学限制或初始姿态不佳的情况下，直接放置物体不可行，因此需要通过双手协作完成物体重定向。然而，现有方法难以同时满足多个竞争性目标（如避障、轨迹平滑、传递可行性等），因此需要一种能协同优化多阶段任务的方法。

Method: 提出BiCompoDiff框架，结合预训练的抓取扩散模型与双手机械臂规划的能量模型（EBM），在反向扩散过程中引入梯度引导以满足多种约束条件（如避障、轨迹平滑、传递可行性和再抓取安全）。采用退火MCMC采样在复合能量景观上进一步优化抓取姿态。

Result: 在多种模拟家庭场景的物体重定向任务中，相比强基线方法，BiCompoDiff的成功率提高超过20%，轨迹平滑度提升最高达37%（以关节位移衡量）。真实世界实验验证了其良好的仿真到现实迁移能力和在复杂场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BiCompoDiff通过融合扩散模型与能量模型，实现了对双手机械臂物体重定向任务的高效、安全且平滑的规划，显著优于现有方法，并具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Bimanual object reorientation - picking an object, handing it over between two arms, and placing it in a desired target pose - is valuable when direct placement from the initial grasp is infeasible due to collisions, kinematic constraints, or poor final orientation. However, achieving this under multiple competing objectives remains challenging. We introduce BiCompoDiff, a compositional diffusion and energy-based framework that jointly optimizes grasp selection, handover, regrasp, and motion planning under multiple constraints. By combining a pretrained grasp diffusion model with bimanual planning energy-based models (EBMs), our method injects gradient guidance during reverse diffusion to enforce collision avoidance, trajectory smoothness (via differentiable inverse kinematics), handover feasibility, and regrasp safety. Annealed MCMC sampling further refines grasp poses over the composite energy landscape. Experiments across diverse simulated household reorientation tasks demonstrate that BiCompoDiff achieves over 20% higher success rates and up to 37% smoother trajectories (measured by joint displacement) compared to strong sampling-based baselines. Real-world validation confirms effective sim-to-real transfer and robust performance on challenging scenes.

</details>


### [196] [VoLN: Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation---Paradigm, Benchmark, and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21400)
*Jiabin Lou,Haopeng Wang,Yuanshuai Wang,Xinyu Liu,Xuxin Lv,Yuxin Guo,Lei Huang,Rongye Shi,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation (VoLN)，将路线信息从语言指令转移到场景内的局部可观测线索，用于视觉主导的长距离导航，并发布了VoLN-UAV基准和VoLN-MLLM基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航依赖任务描述中显式提供的空间先验（如方向、距离），但在无GPS开放环境中缺乏实际部署能力，因此需要一种仅依赖局部视觉线索的导航方法。

Method: 提出VoLN框架，目标通过视觉画面指定，路线信息仅来自可在线感知的场景内线索；构建包含7210个航程的VoLN-UAV基准，涵盖长距离飞行、连续3D运动和大视角变化；设计VoLN-MLLM基线模型，融合自监督视觉特征与结构化语义空间，基于观测历史、目标视图、检索到的视觉-语义标记和本体感知预测短时路点。

Result: 在Test-Unseen五环境分割上，VoLN-MLLM在Easy、Normal和Hard难度下的成功率分别为7.4%、4.5%和1.8%，揭示了长距离证据整合、跨视角目标匹配和闭环稳定性方面的挑战。

Conclusion: VoLN为纯视觉长距离导航提供了新范式，强调对局部可观察线索的在线感知与理解，尽管当前性能有限，但指出了未来改进的关键方向。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) enables embodied agents to follow natural-language instructions. However, route-level instructions commonly encode spatial priors, such as orientation, distance, and layout, that are not explicitly available from onboard sensing at deployment in open, GPS-denied environments. Benchmark performance under such interfaces therefore jointly reflects visual navigation ability and the use of route structure explicitly supplied by the task description. As a complementary formulation, we propose Vision-Only Long-Horizon Navigation (VoLN), which shifts route-relevant information from externally supplied instructions and global guidance to locally observable in-scene cues. In VoLN, goal views specify the destination, while route-relevant information is available only through locally observable in-scene cues that the agent must detect, interpret, and select online. We instantiate VoLN for aerial navigation through VoLN-UAV, a 7,210-episode benchmark that combines long-horizon goal-directed flight, continuous 3D motion, large viewpoint changes, and context-dependent beacon selection. We further provide VoLN-MLLM as an initial reference baseline. It aligns self-supervised visual features with a structured semantic space and predicts short-horizon waypoint segments from observation history, goal views, retrieved visual--semantic tokens, and proprioception. On the five-environment Test-Unseen split, it obtains success rates of 7.4%, 4.5%, and 1.8% on Easy, Normal, and Hard episodes, respectively. These results provide an initial evaluation of VoLN and reveal substantial remaining challenges in long-horizon evidence integration, cross-view goal matching, and closed-loop stability. Project page: https://admire-ljb.github.io/VoLN-UAV/

</details>


### [197] [GLAM-SLAM: Real-time Gaussian Large-scale Mapping via Flow Densification and Spatial Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21416)
*Panagiotis Mermigkas,Argyris Manetas,Petros Maragos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GLAM-SLAM的实时、解耦式高斯点阵SLAM系统，专为大规模户外场景设计，通过结构化稀疏锚点网格和几何流稠密化策略，在长序列中实现了优于现有方法15%的重建质量，同时保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于高斯点阵的单目SLAM系统在短序列上表现良好，但在长序列中存在实时性差或GPU内存需求过高的问题，限制了其在实际大尺度场景中的应用。因此，需要一种能够实现实时、可扩展且内存高效的SLAM系统。

Method: 采用轻量级特征跟踪前端，并引入结构化稀疏锚点网格表示用于建图；提出基于几何流的稠密化锚定策略以满足3D高斯点阵的初始化需求；将建图视为多场景问题，通过MLP初始化引入空间归纳偏置，实现局部高斯分布的生成。

Result: 在KITTI Odometry、Oxford RobotCar和M'alaga等长序列数据集上进行了评估，实验表明该系统在重建质量上比次优方法提升了15%，同时保持实时运行能力，并能扩展到更长序列。

Conclusion: GLAM-SLAM通过解耦跟踪与建图、引入结构化表示和分区策略，有效解决了现有高斯点阵SLAM在大规模、长序列场景下的效率与质量平衡问题，具备良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Existing Gaussian-splatting-based monocular Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) systems are either tailored to short sequences, are not real-time, or suffer from prohibitive GPU memory requirements, limiting their applicability in realistic, long-horizon scenarios. To address this, we present GLAM-SLAM, a real-time, decoupled Gaussian-splatting SLAM system designed for large-scale outdoor scenes. We ensure lightweight tracking using a robust, feature-based SLAM frontend, while for mapping, we adopt a structured, sparse anchor grid representation that ensures scalable operation and maintains scene coherence across long-term sequences. To satisfy the dense initialization requirements of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), we introduce a geometry-based flow-densification anchoring strategy using epipolar constraints. Furthermore, by treating mapping as a multi-scene problem, we propose a scene-partitioning strategy that introduces a strong spatial inductive bias via MLP initializations to generate localized Gaussians. We evaluate our system on the challenging, long-sequence KITTI Odometry, Oxford RobotCar, and M'alaga datasets. Extensive ablations and comparisons demonstrate a 15% improvement in reconstruction quality over the second-best performer, while maintaining real-time performance and the ability to scale to longer sequences. Code is publicly available for the benefit of the community.

</details>


### [198] [GS-Agent: Creating 4D Physical Worlds With Generative Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21522)
*Hongxin Zhang,Chunru Lin,Junyan Li,Zhou Xian,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Agent是一个端到端的多智能体框架，通过结合物理引擎，从自然语言生成动态、真实且可控的4D物理世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖人工创建4D世界，费时费力；现有生成模型难以保证物理合理性和可控性。因此需要一种能自动化且符合物理规律的方法。

Method: 提出GS-Agent，将任务分解为实体管理（3D资产、材质、位置、运动）和渲染配置（相机、光照），多个专业智能体通过代码与物理引擎交互，利用多模态反馈协作迭代构建4D世界。

Result: 实验表明GS-Agent能生成包含液体、可变形物体和刚体之间丰富交互的多样化、物理合理的4D世界，并实现电影级相机与光照控制。

Conclusion: GS-Agent为4D世界生成提供了新范式，有望推动创意内容生成和物理AI的发展。

Abstract: Creating dynamic and physically realistic 4D worlds from natural language descriptions is both fascinating and challenging. Traditional computer graphics methods rely on manual creation, requiring extensive human effort to fine-tune materials, motions, and visual fidelity. Recent advances in generative foundation models have sparked interest in learning to generate such 4D worlds from large-scale data; however, existing methods still struggle to ensure physical plausibility and controllability. In this work, we take a different path by leveraging foundation models to construct an agentic system that emulates how humans traditionally create 4D worlds, yet automates the entire process. We present GS-Agent, an end-to-end multi-agent framework that integrates physics engines in the loop to generate realistic, dynamic, and controllable 4D physical worlds from natural language. Inspired by how humans build 4D worlds, GS-Agent decomposes the task into entity management, covering 3D asset curation, material tuning, placement, and motion control, and rendering configuration, including camera and lighting manipulation. Multiple agents with distinct expertise interact with the physics engine via code, seek multimodal feedback, and collaborate to iteratively construct 4D worlds that align with the given descriptions. Experimental results show that GS-Agent effectively converts natural language into diverse and physically plausible 4D worlds exhibiting rich interactions among liquids, deformable objects, and rigid bodies, while achieving cinematic camera and lighting control. We envision GS-Agent as a foundation for a new paradigm in 4D world generation, empowering creative content creation and physical AI. Project page at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/gs-agent/

</details>


### [199] [Beyond Episodic Evaluation: Memory Architectural Bottlenecks in Sequential Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21571)
*Zikui Cai,Kaushal Janga,Tan Dat Dao,Seungjae Lee,Shivin Dass,Mingyo Seo,Kaiyu Yue,Mintong Kang,Nandhu Pillai,Monte Hoover,Aadi Palnitkar,Ruchit Rawal,Ruijie Zheng,Bo Li,Yuke Zhu,Roberto Martín-Martín,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了具身问答（EQA）中连续任务下的记忆架构问题，提出基于空间定位的结构化记忆可有效提升机器人在连续多问题场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的机器人需要在连续交互中积累、保留和重用信息，而传统EQA采用 episodic 模式，忽略了对序列记忆机制的研究。

Method: 通过在模拟环境中评估不同记忆架构在连续多问题、同一场景下的表现，并引入将视觉观测映射到度量3D几何的结构化空间记忆。

Result: 实验表明，仅保留已探索区域信息的记忆不足以支持后续问答；基于短时程训练的模型存在上下文时间错配问题；而空间定位的视觉记忆能同时提高回答准确率并降低导航成本。

Conclusion: 结构化的、空间定位的记忆架构对于实现连续、智能的物理环境操作至关重要，是解决EQA序列任务的关键。

Abstract: Embodied question answering (EQA) is traditionally evaluated under an episodic formulation, where agents solve each task independently and reset internal state between episodes. However, real-world robots operate continuously and must accumulate, retain, and selectively reuse information acquired from prior interactions. Despite this practical requirement, the architectural mechanisms needed to support sequential memory in EQA remain underexplored. In this work, we investigate how different memory architectures behave when EQA agents are evaluated sequentially, with multiple questions answered in the same scene while memory is carried forward across queries. We find that simply preserving existing memory is often insufficient. Agents that retain only traversability information, such as 2D occupancy maps, remember where the robot has explored but not the visual-semantic evidence needed for later questions. Agents trained on short-horizon episodic data face a different challenge: when exposed to continuous, multi-query histories, their inherited context suffers from severe temporal mismatch, rather than forming a reusable scene representation. To overcome this architectural bottleneck, we highlight the necessity of structured, spatially grounded memory: architectures that map persistent visual observations onto metric 3D geometry preserve visual-semantic evidence in a coherent scene representation. Extensive experiments in simulated environments reveal that this form of memory breaks the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff in sequential settings, simultaneously achieving higher answer accuracy and lower navigation costs. We further validate these findings on a real-world mobile robot, demonstrating that spatially grounded visual memory is critical for enabling continuous, intelligent operation in physical environments.

</details>


### [200] [Scale Up Strategically: Learning Compositional Generalization via Bias-Aware Evaluation and Data Collection for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21582)
*Yu Qi,Zhang Ye,Xinyi Xu,Yuxuan Lu,Amitoj Sandhu,Boce Hu,Haojie Huang,Jonathan Tremblay,Lawson L. S. Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种诊断框架，用于识别机器人在理解组合性语言指令时对特定语义因素（如颜色、物体、动作等）的依赖偏差，并提出了两种量化指标（FDR和FDH），发现颜色最常被用作捷径，而动词和大小最易被忽略；通过针对弱 grounding 因素进行有偏的数据采集策略，可在仅使用一半演示数据的情况下提升策略的泛化性和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练策略在理解多样化语言指令时常依赖显著特征走捷径，而非真正语义接地，导致组合泛化能力差，因此需要一种可诊断并纠正这种偏差的框架。

Method: 提出一种诊断框架，将指令分解为多个语义因子（如颜色、动作、物体等），定义了指令因子偏差概念，并通过Factor Dominance Rate (FDR) 和 Factor Dominance Hierarchy (FDH) 两个指标量化各因子的主导程度和全局排序。

Result: 在六个基础策略上验证显示因子主导顺序一致：颜色 ≥ 物体 ≥ 空间 ≥ 动作 ≥ 大小；颜色最占主导，动作和大小最弱；基于诊断设计的偏向弱因子的数据采集策略在仿真和真实机器人上均优于基线方法，且仅需一半演示数据。

Conclusion: 该诊断框架能有效揭示语言接地中的因子偏差，指导更高效的数据收集，从而提升策略的组合泛化能力和样本效率。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is essential for robot to follow diverse instructions. However, pretrained policies are known to take shortcuts, deferring to salient cues rather than grounding language. We introduce a diagnostic framework that localizes this failure to individual \textit{instruction factors}, \textit{e.g.,} reusable semantic components such as color, verb, object, size, and spatial attribute. Our framework formalizes instruction factor bias, the tendency of fine-tuned policies to over-rely on dominant factors as shortcuts, and quantifies it through two metrics: Factor Dominance Rate (FDR), capturing pairwise bias between factors, and Factor Dominance Hierarchy (FDH), aggregating these into a global ranking. Evaluation on six foundation policies reveals broadly consistent ordering, \textit{i.e.}, color $\geq$ object $\geq$ spatial $\geq$ verb $\geq$ size, with color dominant, and verb and size most under-grounded. We further show the diagnosis is actionable: a bias-aware data collection strategy that reallocates a fixed budget toward under-grounded factors outperforms baselines in simulation and on a real robot using half the demonstrations, thereby enabling more sample-efficient and generalizable policy learning.

</details>


### [201] [AXIS: A Growable Community-Driven Data Engine for Scalable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21588)
*Mengfei Zhao,Dihong Huang,Yikai Tang,Peihao Li,Mingxuan Yan,Ruiqi Zhuang,Yanjia Huang,Jie Wang,Hai Zhai,Tony Zhou,Rui Zhang,Zhexi Luo,Yuchen Huang,Jianfei Yang,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AXIS是一个可扩展的社区驱动数据引擎和基准，用于大规模机器人学习，支持浏览器远程操作、自动任务生成与验证，并通过自动化处理将社区收集的演示转化为训练就绪数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略的学习依赖高质量演示，但数据采集难以扩展，通常受限于专用硬件、集中式操作员或固定任务集。因此需要一个更开放、可扩展的数据引擎来推动机器人学习的发展。

Method: 提出AXIS，采用基于浏览器的遥操作进行大规模示范采集，自动创建并验证新操作任务，利用自动成功检测、质量过滤、轨迹平滑以及视觉和物理增强技术将社区数据转换为训练就绪格式，并构建系统性的保留任务快照评估协议。

Result: AXIS数据集包含207个多样化任务和超过5万条轨迹；在统一评估框架下测试视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，持续在AXIS上预训练使π₀.₅整体成功率提升5.8%，相比RoboCasa365预训练模型提升37.3%，且在布局、传感器噪声和相机扰动下表现最佳。

Conclusion: AXIS提供了一个可扩展、社区驱动的机器人学习数据基础设施，显著提升了机器人策略的泛化能力和训练效率，展示了数据规模对性能提升的关键作用。

Abstract: Learning effective robot manipulation policies requires diverse, high-quality demonstrations, yet existing data pipelines are often difficult to scale because they rely on specialized hardware, centralized operators, or fixed task suites. We present AXIS, a growable community-driven data engine and benchmark for scalable robot learning, which enables browser-based teleoperation for large-scale demonstration collection, automatically generates and validates new manipulation tasks, and transforms community-collected demonstrations into training-ready data through automated success checking, quality filtering, trajectory smoothing, and visual and physics-based augmentation. The AXIS dataset currently contains 207 diverse tasks and 50K+ trajectories. Meanwhile, AXIS organizes data into task snapshots and evaluates policies with a systematic held-out protocol. We compare vision-language-action (VLA) policies under a unified AXIS evaluation suite and analyze scaling behavior across different data volumes. Continual pretraining on AXIS substantially improves the overall success rate of $π_{0.5}$ by 5.8%, outperforms the model pretrained on RoboCasa365 by 37.3%, and exhibits consistent scaling with increasing data volume, with the largest gains observed under layout, sensor-noise, and camera perturbations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AINTMA是一个基于多智能体的智能测试管理架构，通过六类AI代理在云原生环境中实现自主质量保障，显著提升测试效率与软件质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试管理难以适应现代分布式云环境下的快速、智能决策需求，亟需具备自适应能力的智能化系统来提升软件质量保障水平。

Method: 提出AINTMA架构，集成六个专用AI代理（测试发现、风险评估、强化学习优先级排序、执行编排、生成式质量智能和云安全监控），通过零信任API网关和加密通信实现在微服务基础设施上的安全协同；其中强化学习代理将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，生成式代理利用大语言模型生成质量报告和建议。

Result: 在12个异构项目中18个月的评估显示：测试优先级准确率达88.4%（APFD），比随机方法（51.2%）和最佳商业基线（82.1%）更高；测试周期缩短43%；缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；9个月回本，投资回报率达340%；系统可扩展至5万以上测试用例，响应时间低于400ms，生成模块开发者评分为4.3/5.0。

Conclusion: AINTMA证明了结合多智能体协作、生成式智能与安全连接的代理式AI能从根本上推动大规模云环境中的软件质量管理进步。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [203] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统评估了大语言模型水印技术在医学领域的性能影响，发现现有水印方案可能导致临床文本生成中的语义错误、术语幻觉和图像结果误报等问题，强调需建立领域特定的评估标准以确保医疗应用安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于医学文本对语义精确性要求极高，通用水印技术可能引入严重错误，但目前缺乏针对医学领域的水印影响评估，因此需要开展专门研究。

Method: 评估了5种水印方案在11个大语言模型和7个视觉语言模型上的表现，涵盖多种单模态与多模态临床推理任务，并引入由医学专家验证的审计流程，评估医学推理质量、术语准确性和幻觉现象。

Result: 水印技术会显著降低医学文本生成质量，引发词汇损坏、术语幻觉、图像发现的错误归因或遗漏等多类故障；现有聚合指标和非领域特定评估方法容易掩盖这些临床重要问题。

Conclusion: 在医学等高风险领域，必须采用领域特定的评估方法来检测水印带来的实际退化，现行通用基准不足以保障水印模型的安全部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [204] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的浏览器扩展，通过结合Transformer嵌入和语言学特征的混合模型识别点击诱饵，提供实时警告、可信度评分和文章摘要，F1得分为91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决网络上日益严重的点击诱饵问题，帮助用户避免被误导性标题吸引而浪费时间或受到信息误导。

Method: 采用混合机器学习架构，结合Transformer模型的嵌入表示与人工设计的语言学特征，并引入自定义的“baitness”评分机制；使用XGBoost模型进行分类，并在开放数据集上训练和评估。

Result: 模型在综合公开数据集上达到了91%的F1分数；浏览器扩展能实时预警点击诱饵，提供可解释的预测结果和内容摘要（clickbait spoiler）。

Conclusion: 该工具不仅能高效识别点击诱饵，还能通过透明化预测依据和内容预披露提升用户的信息素养和浏览决策能力。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [205] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了语言模型在重复运行中产生不同答案的变异性是否能揭示其知识盲区。通过自一致性（多数投票）可获得准确的每题不确定性估计，但无法捕捉跨题目结构；而多样化集成模型则能有效揭示模型未知的知识结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型输出变异性是否能够反映其不确定性，特别是是否存在跨问题的相关性结构，从而更好地理解模型的知识边界。

Method: 比较两种设置：同一模型运行100次（温度τ=1）与24个不同大语言模型各运行一次（τ=0）。使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论分析特征值，区分信号与采样噪声，并评估自一致性和集成方法的表现。

Result: 单一模型最多只有一个维度显著超出噪声水平，表明缺乏可检测的跨题结构；而在集成模型中，有四个特征值明显高于噪声阈值，且在控制难度的伯努利零假设下极不可能出现，说明仅多样化集成能揭示模型未知结构。

Conclusion: 自一致性可用于估计单个问题的不确定性，但不能揭示跨问题的知识结构；只有多样化的模型集成才能有效揭示语言模型真正不知道的内容。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [206] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了JAXBench，一个专为Google Cloud TPU设计的AI生成内核优化基准套件，包含50个JAX工作负载，用于推动TPU上的自动性能优化研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU内核优化已有成熟基准推动进展，但TPU领域缺乏类似标准，因此需要构建一个面向TPU、特别是针对AI生成代码优化的权威基准套件。

Method: 构建了包含17个来自MaxText库的真实生产级ML算子和33个从KernelBench迁移并调整问题规模以适配TPU v6e MXU利用率的算子的基准集；使用Gemini 3 Flash等四种反馈驱动方法生成Pallas内核，并评估其在正确性和性能上的表现。

Result: 实验表明，在稀疏文档化的DSL（如Pallas）中，特定于目标的上下文比模型规模更重要；结合TPU文档可将单样本正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，并在全部50个基准中实现1.28倍的几何平均加速；Autocomp的beam-search流程达到1.36倍XLA加速；在8个手工调优内核上，Autocomp达到1.60倍XLA加速，接近Tokamax上限（2.08倍），但在特殊注意力机制上仍有差距。

Conclusion: 高质量的TPU内核优化仍具挑战性，JAXBench及其评估框架与基线结果已公开发布，旨在促进开源社区在此方向上的进一步研究与贡献。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [207] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap是一种无需训练的框架，通过动态连续验证和草稿引导解码机制，有效解决扩散大语言模型中的联合概率依赖误差问题，实现可靠的并行解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散大语言模型在并行解码时因过于保守的置信度阈值导致冗余去噪迭代和推理速度不佳，主要受限于联合概率依赖误差（JPDE）。

Method: 提出DC-Leap框架，采用动态连续验证策略引入严格有序的因果约束来逐步验证token依赖关系，并结合草稿引导解码机制，利用草稿跳跃式扩展上下文以提供前瞻信息。

Result: 在标准基准测试中实现了显著加速效果，最长序列生成任务上最高达53.19倍加速，在结合KV-Cache时可达105.02倍加速，同时保持相当的生成质量。

Conclusion: DC-Leap能有效消除JPDE影响，在中等置信度下实现可靠且高效的并行解码，显著提升dLLMs的推理速度，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [208] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench 是一个用于评估 AI 代理在开放-ended 场景下优化大语言模型推理速度能力的新基准，结果显示当前代理虽能改进基线，但探索策略多样性不足，性能仍低于简单超参数搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多评估固定流程或窄动作空间中的 AI 代理，难以判断其是否具备真正的优化能力还是仅依赖记忆化方案；因此需要一个更开放、更具挑战性的评估环境。

Method: 提出 InferenceBench，要求代理部署兼容 OpenAI 的推理服务，并在两小时内针对预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发吞吐量等瓶颈优化 LLM 推理速度；包含四种优化场景，在 H100 GPU 上进行测试。

Result: 15 种前沿代理配置均能超越朴素 PyTorch 基线（最高提升 8.08 倍），部分接近或超过默认设置的 vLLM 等推理引擎（4.05 倍），但仍低于相同时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（最高 11.53 倍）；轨迹分析显示代理大多收敛于单一推理框架，探索配置少，重复测量和调参多。

Conclusion: 当前 AI 代理在开放-ended AI 工程任务中受限于提出多样化配置和系统评估的能力，而非领域知识缺乏；InferenceBench 揭示了代理在真实工程优化中实现突破性进展的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [209] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DecodeShare的新协议，用于识别大语言模型在解码时跨任务共享的低维子空间，并验证其因果作用。实验表明，干扰该子空间对决策性能的影响显著大于干扰其他子空间，且该共享子空间在激活引导中具有实际意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在KV缓存推理下，大语言模型在解码阶段是否存在任务通用的结构尚不明确，本文旨在探究解码时隐藏状态中是否存在跨任务共享的结构及其功能角色。

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议，通过识别解码时跨任务共享的低维子空间，并在解码过程中移除该子空间以测试其因果影响；同时分析该子空间在激活引导中的作用。

Result: 干扰发现的共享子空间比干扰prefill阶段提取或随机子空间更显著地降低决策性能；该共享子空间与常见的引导方向重叠，投影出该子空间可分离功能角色，且解码时评估引导向量比基于prefill的方法提供更可靠的信号。

Conclusion: 尽管紧凑，解码时共享子空间在解码阶段充当高杠杆的因果通道，对理解大语言模型的任务通用机制和优化激活引导具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [210] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PlanE的框架，用于通过数据分解、指令调优和提示推理来增强大语言模型在特定任务上的能力，并引入了DTI规划器以优化基础模型及其组合的选择，实验证明了该方法在不同数据集和模型下的有效性与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模语言模型在特定任务上需要大量指令调优数据而导致标注成本高以及缺乏针对特定任务优化方法的问题。

Method: 提出了PlanE框架，包括数据分解、指令调优和提示推理三个阶段，并设计了数据-调优-推理（DTI）规划器，用于选择最优的基础大模型及其DTI组合，以提升构建效率。

Result: 实验表明，PlanE在相同基模型下不同数据集和相同数据集下不同基模型上均有效，且DTI规划器在不同优化目标下具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PlanE框架能有效降低对大规模标注数据的依赖，提升大模型在特定任务上的定制化效率，DTI规划器有助于自动化选择最优配置，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [211] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成式语言模型的个性化消息传递框架，并发布了SDR-Bench和SDR-Arena两个公开数据集，用于可重复的大规模生成式个性化研究；实验显示当前前沿模型在真实销售场景中未能有效区分成功与失败的沟通，存在个性化性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的个性化评估主要集中在发送者自身适应，缺乏对第三方接收者行为影响的可复现评测；需要一个能衡量生成内容是否真正诱导目标行为的两方个性化评估框架。

Method: 采用Kamenica与Gentzkow（2011）的贝叶斯说服框架，构建面向生成代理的个性化建模方法，并在销售场景中实例化；利用包含6,279个客户成功案例的SDR-Bench数据集，通过时间受限的模拟防止未来数据泄露，评估不同模型在真实行为日志上的表现。

Result: 在多个前沿大模型和深度研究代理上观察到一致的个性化性能 plateau，在财富100强科技公司样本中，没有任何模型能统计显著地区分成功与失败的外展消息；实地部署中，48%的模型生成内容被专业销售人员认为可立即使用，专家评分相关性达Pearson 0.82。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在实现经典意义上的个性化（即影响第三方行为）方面仍存在局限，尽管生成内容具有一定实用性，但尚未突破性能瓶颈；所提出的框架和数据集为未来生成式个性化研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [212] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为对话批评引导采样（DCGS）的框架，通过建模对抗性对话为马尔可夫决策过程，利用价值和遗憾批评器在推理时重加权候选响应，从而提升语言模型在多轮对话中的安全性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全框架在多轮对话中忽略对话轨迹，难以准确判断用户意图，导致对有害请求的误判或被攻击者利用。需要一种能动态理解用户意图并适应上下文的安全机制。

Method: 将对抗性对话建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在每个对话轮次学习基于值和遗憾的批评器（token级和utterance级），通过动作值批评器对候选响应进行评分，并在推理时重新加权生成策略，形式上证明该方法近似于基础策略的指数倾斜，保证预期回报提升。

Result: 在CARES-18k、WildJailbreak、Redbench和Harmbench数据集上，DCGS优于强基线和前沿模型，在对抗性对话任务中表现出更强的鲁棒性，并可在无需微调的情况下迁移到其他模型以提升其安全性。

Conclusion: DCGS通过结合对话历史动态推断用户意图，在不依赖固定安全规则的前提下有效提升了语言模型在多轮交互中的安全性和泛化能力，是一种可迁移、理论上有保障的增强方法。

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue. Instead of applying a fixed rule about what is or is not safe, DCGS learns what the user's intent is likely to be based on the full conversational history and generates responses accordingly. Formally, we model adversarial dialogue as a Markov Decision Process and learn value and regret-based critics at both the individual token and utterance (full response) levels, scoring candidate responses via an action-value critic. We prove that this inference-time reweighting approximates exponential tilting of the base policy, guaranteeing improvement in expected return for any finite candidate pool, a property that group-relative objectives do not exhibit. Evaluated on CARES-18k, WildJailbreak, Redbench, and Harmbench, DCGS outperforms strong robust baselines and frontier models on adversarial dialogue tasks. DCGS also transfers to frontier models, improving their robustness without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [213] [Incomplete Prompt Jailbreaks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20473)
*Yeonjea Kim,Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了不完整提示越狱（IPJ）的概念，系统分析了大型语言模型在面对不完整有害提示时的行为，并探索了通过功能神经元实现更精确防御的可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于开放权重的大规模语言模型在句子补全任务中容易受到不完整有害提示的影响，需要对这一现象进行形式化和系统研究。

Method: 通过实证分析不同类型的吸引子如何引发有害续写，识别出终止神经元和延续神经元，并探讨其在句子补全中的作用。

Result: 发现模型通常会延迟拒绝直到句子结束；基于参数调优的训练方法无法有效泛化到不同内容领域和吸引子类型；识别出两个关键功能神经元。

Conclusion: 功能神经元的识别为实现更精细、更鲁棒的IPJ防御提供了新方向，神经元级干预具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly released as open-weight models with safeguards against harmful requests. Nevertheless, sentence completion remains vulnerable to incomplete harmful prompts. In this work, we formalize this phenomenon as incomplete prompt jailbreaks (IPJ) and provide a systematic empirical characterization of when and how incomplete prompts elicit harmful continuations. We analyze diverse attractor types associated with incomplete sentence continuation and show that LLMs systematically delay refusal until sentence termination. We further demonstrate that training models to refuse incomplete harmful prompts via parameter tuning is insufficient, failing to generalize across both content domains and attractor types. To enable fine-grained control, we identify two functional neurons: termination and continuation neurons. By clarifying their roles in sentence completion, we highlight the potential of neuron-level interventions for more precise and robust IPJ defenses.

</details>


### [214] [VeriSimpl: Robust Optimization Modeling from Natural Language using Simplification-based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20474)
*Sumaya Abdul Rahman,Seckhen Ariel Andrade Cuellar,Ghani Raissov,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriSimpl是一种基于大语言模型的求解器框架，通过简化验证策略将自然语言问题准确转化为优化建模，提升了现有方法的精度并提供高精度自验证信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言到优化建模的转化方法难以确保生成的公式正确反映任务意图，即使代码可执行也可能存在语义偏差。

Method: 提出VeriSimpl框架，利用求解器生成关于候选公式的简化诊断查询，使大语言模型能在固定全局上下文中沿约束和决策变量等维度进行局部推理，实现基于简化的正确性验证。

Result: 在多种优化基准测试中，该方法相比现有技术显著提高了转化准确性，并提供了新颖的高精度自我验证信号。

Conclusion: 基于简化的验证机制有效提升了自然语言到优化形式化过程的正确性和可靠性，为语言驱动的优化建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces can greatly benefit the accessibility and usability of optimization modeling, and recent advances in large language models (LLMs) show promise in automatically translating textual problem descriptions into executable solver formulations. However, a key challenge for existing approaches is to ensure that the inferred formulation correctly implements the intended task, even if it may execute without errors. We introduce VeriSimpl, a solver LLM framework for robust natural-language-to-optimization formalization. Our approach is based on the idea of simplification-based verification, where the optimization solver is leveraged to generate simplified diagnostic queries about a candidate formulation to allow the LLM to tractably reason about the correctness of the formulation with respect to the task description. We present such simplification strategies along different dimensions with respect to problem constraints and decision variables, which allow the LLM to reason locally under fixed global contexts. Evaluations on a range of optimization benchmarks show how our approach provides consistent improvements in accuracy over existing methods, while also providing a novel high-precision self-verification signal.

</details>


### [215] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SonicSampler的统一Triton内核套件，将大语言模型推理中的采样流程完全融合到一个固定且工作负载感知的执行模型中，支持动态批量处理和CUDA Graph兼容性，在异构推测解码任务中相比现有最先进基线最高实现16倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM推理采样实现通常只加速部分流水线、依赖多次内核启动或假设批次内采样行为同质化，限制了对动态服务工作负载的支持，并阻碍了高效的CUDA Graph执行。

Method: 设计了一套面向Tile的Triton内核，垂直融合完整的采样流水线（包括logit处理、令牌选择和推测验证等操作），并提出一种新颖的分层两阶段top-k算法，利用LLM输出的低熵特性实现对大词汇表的高效选择。

Result: SonicSampler在多种异构推测解码工作负载下，相比当前最先进的基线最高实现了16倍的加速，其核心的top-k算法比竞争基线快达10倍，同时保持对动态每请求采样行为和CUDA Graph的完全支持。

Conclusion: SonicSampler通过统一、融合的内核设计，解决了现有采样系统在效率、灵活性和兼容性方面的局限，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了一个可扩展且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [216] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Multi-Sensor Physical Hazard Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20476)
*Faizan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 五种大语言模型在评估多传感器物理危险数据时，对多传感器联合评估场景普遍未能发出预警信号，但在单传感器阈值违规场景中表现接近完美，结果提示这些模型在物理安全监控系统中的部署需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在多传感器物理危险数据判断中的可靠性，特别是在复合风险场景下的预警能力，以指导其在实际安全监测系统中的应用。

Method: 通过1,800次API调用（温度设为0.0），测试五个大语言模型在60种场景下的表现，涵盖三类任务：多传感器联合评估、响应适当性、模式消歧；比较结构化表格与纯文本输入的效果。

Result: 所有模型在多传感器同时升高但未达个体安全限的场景下均未发出预警（Category A得分接近零），而在单传感器场景中准确率极高（Category B Q1得分0.975-1.000）；结构化输入未显现出一致优势，ChatGPT-4o在纯文本输入下表现更优（p = 0.001）。

Conclusion: 当前测试的大语言模型在处理低于个体阈值的多传感器联合风险时存在明显缺陷，缺乏预防性预警能力，限制了其在物理安全监控系统中的直接应用。

Abstract: We present an empirical benchmark evaluating how five large language models assess multisensor physical hazard data. Testing 60 scenarios across three categories - multi-sensor joint assessment, response proportionality, and pattern disambiguation - with 1,800 API calls at temperature 0.0, we find that all tested models consistently produced no precautionary warning signal across the tested scenarios where multiple sensors are simultaneously elevated below their individual safety limits, while achieving near-perfect accuracy on single-sensor threshold violations. All five models (ChatGPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek, Kimi, Llama 3.1 8B) score near zero on Category A multi-sensor scenarios (Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592) compared to strong performance on single-sensor scenarios (Category B Q1: 0.975-1.000). Structured tabular formatting shows no consistent advantage over plain prose; ChatGPT-4o performs significantly better under prose (p = 0.001). These findings have direct implications for practitioners deploying the tested models in physical safety monitoring systems.

</details>


### [217] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein距离的半监督框架\algo{}，用于解决文本属性图（TAGs）在结合大语言模型时的可扩展性、标签稀缺和生成人类可读文本的问题。该方法通过双路径编码器和协同自训练机制融合图与文本特征，并采用WSD驱动的图草图和关键词引导的文本生成，实现了高效且有效的TAG表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAG表示学习方法在结合大语言模型时面临可扩展性瓶颈，难以捕捉图与文本模态间的复杂交互，且在半监督场景下存在标签稀缺问题，同时无法生成适用于LLM任务的人类可读文本。

Method: \algo{}框架包含：1）图-文本协同编码模块，使用图感知与图无关的双路径编码器进行协同自训练以获取可靠伪标签；2）基于Wasserstein距离的图草图算法，实现理论保证的图压缩；3）基于聚类关键词提取的低成本LLM文本生成模块，为压缩节点生成连贯且可读的摘要。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，\algo{}在GNN和LLM两类下游任务中均实现了最先进的性能-压缩权衡，显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在低标签率设置下表现突出。

Conclusion: \algo{}为大规模文本属性图的学习提供了一个统一、高效且可解释的解决方案，有效平衡了模型性能与计算成本，推动了图分析与大语言模型的深度融合。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统探讨了影响大型语言模型欺骗性输出检测性能的多种因素，发现训练数据的选择和谎言类型对可检测性有显著影响，表明欺骗检测高度依赖于表示方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的探测器在跨领域场景中表现不佳，特别是针对不同类型的谎言时缺乏泛化能力，因此需要系统研究影响检测效果的因素。

Method: 通过扩展包含多种欺骗类型（如虚构、遗漏和夸大）的训练数据，分析七种探测器在表示深度、探测器表达能力、稀疏特征表示和谎言分类上的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，最优表示深度因数据集而异，更具表达力的探测器仅在部分情况下优于线性基线，稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐藏状态表现相似，且训练数据和谎言类型显著影响检测性能。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测性能高度依赖于表示方式，训练数据和谎言类型的选取至关重要，未来研究需关注多样化和代表性的数据构建。

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [219] [Enabling Scalable Topology Inference in Distribution Systems via Constrained Multi-Source Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20480)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Muhao Guo,Jiaqi Wu,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束推断的配电系统拓扑识别框架，通过融合异构数据、空间可行性和物理约束来修正基础拓扑，实现高精度、可扩展且操作一致的拓扑恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于公用事业数据异构且不完善，维持准确的配电系统拓扑连接记录具有挑战性；现有方法在密集馈线和元数据不一致情况下可靠性不足。

Method: 将拓扑识别建模为约束推断问题，在实用程序提供的基础拓扑上进行局部重构，结合电气测量、空间信息和物理运行约束，并引入反证驱动的可靠性指标评估推断连接的置信度。

Result: 在与美国大型电力公司合作的三个包含8000多个AMI电表的馈线上验证，拓扑重建准确率超过95%，计算量显著低于全局推断方法；相关性方法在城市密集馈线中产生歧义，而融合多源约束的方法表现稳健。

Conclusion: 融合异构证据与物理和空间约束的局部化约束推断框架可在实际部署条件下实现高效、鲁棒且可扩展的配电拓扑识别，支持电网运行与可观测性维护。

Abstract: Accurate distribution system topology is essential for outage localization, voltage analytics, and operation of distribution grids, yet maintaining reliable connectivity records remains challenging in practice due to heterogeneous and imperfect utility data. Existing topology identification methods often rely primarily on electrical similarity or spatial records alone, which become unreliable in dense feeders and under inconsistent metadata conditions. This paper formulates distribution topology identification as a constrained inference problem that refines a utility-provided base topology using heterogeneous evidence while enforcing spatial feasibility and physical operational constraints. Instead of reconstructing connectivity from scratch, the proposed framework detects inconsistent assignments, performs localized reconnection within constrained neighborhoods to ensure scalability, and iteratively enforces physical feasibility to produce operationally consistent topology estimates. In addition, a falsification-driven reliability metric evaluates how strongly each inferred connection is supported relative to alternative feasible assignments, enabling utilities to prioritize verification efforts while preserving system-wide observability. The framework is validated using operational data from three feeders comprising more than $8{,}000$ AMI meters in collaboration with a large U.S. utility. Results demonstrate over $95\%$ topology reconstruction accuracy while significantly reducing computational effort compared with global inference approaches. The study further shows that correlation-based methods alone produce ambiguous assignments in dense urban feeders, whereas combining electrical measurements with spatial and operational constraints enables robust and scalable topology recovery under realistic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [220] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的本地-云端协作路由框架CARGO，利用本地大语言模型在推理时对多样化提示的响应一致性作为信任信号，结合贝叶斯早停和轻量级校准，实现高效、自适应的计算卸载，在多种任务和模型上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的本地-云端协作方法通常依赖训练好的路由器或协作感知微调，限制了其在不同操作环境下的适应性与通用性，因此需要一种无需训练、更灵活的路由机制。

Method: 提出CARGO框架，通过提示词变化采样估计本地模型的响应一致性，使用贝叶斯早停控制不确定性，并通过部署时轻量级校准支持任意协作比例，完全无需训练。

Result: 在多种推理和问答任务、不同规模和类型的本地大模型（包括预训练和微调模型）上，CARGO持续优于其他无需训练的基线方法，并在多个场景下超越有监督的训练型路由器。

Conclusion: 有效的本地-云端协作可直接源于本地模型内在的响应行为一致性，无需额外训练路由器，CARGO为资源受限下的大模型部署提供了高效且自适应的新范式。

Abstract: Local-cloud collaboration is a practical way to deploy large language models under resource constraints, but existing methods often rely on trained routers or collaboration-aware finetuning that tie routing behavior to a particular operating regime. In this work, we show that such training may be unnecessary: the local model's own inference-time agreement across sampled responses already provides a strong signal for deciding when to trust local execution and when to offload to a stronger cloud model. We propose CARGO, a training-free routing framework that estimates this agreement through prompt-varied sampling, applies Bayesian early stopping for sample-efficient uncertainty control, and supports arbitrary target collaboration ratios through lightweight deployment-time calibration. Across diverse reasoning and question-answering tasks, multiple local LLM families and scales, and both pretrained and finetuned local models, CARGO consistently outperforms other training-free baselines and in several settings surpasses supervised learned routers. These results suggest that effective and adaptable local-cloud collaboration can emerge directly from the local model's intrinsic response behavior, without requiring an additional trained router.

</details>


### [221] [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20482)
*Seungbin Yang,Chaewoon Ki,Dohyun Lee,Jaegul Choo,ChaeHun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PersonaTrail基准和PACMem框架，用于评估和提升个性化网络代理在开放网页环境中基于浏览历史推断用户偏好和记忆信息的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能捕捉用户指令不明确时，代理需从原始浏览历史中推断缺失上下文的个性化需求。

Method: 提出PersonaTrail基准，利用真实的浏览轨迹作为用户历史，并设计Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem) 框架，将浏览历史分解为事实记忆和偏好记忆两种结构化记忆，以支持个性化导航。

Result: 实验表明，PACMem在两项任务上均持续优于现有的基于记忆的基线方法。

Conclusion: PACMem结合结构化记忆机制有效提升了代理在个性化网页操作中的性能，验证了利用真实浏览历史进行个性化建模的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled web agents to autonomously execute complex tasks. In practice, users frequently provide underspecified instructions, requiring agents to infer the missing context from their raw browsing histories. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this form of personalization, as they either restrict tasks to fully explicit prompts or abstract web interaction history into simplified forms. To bridge this gap, we introduce PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents operating in a managed open web environment. By leveraging realistic browsing trajectories as user history, PersonaTrail evaluates an agent's ability to infer user preferences and recall information from past browsing sessions. We further propose Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem), a framework that decomposes raw browsing histories into two types of structured memory: factual memories that summarize individual sessions and preference memories that distill recurring behavioral patterns. At inference time, the agent retrieves the most relevant entries from these memories to guide personalized navigation. Extensive experiments show that PACMem consistently outperforms existing memory-based baselines on both tasks.

</details>


### [222] [Tractable Hierarchical Control of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20483)
*Max Scribner,Antonio Vergari,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在多项式时间内计算满足有限持续时间LR(k)文法的方法，从而高效地约束大语言模型生成符合形式语法约束的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将大语言模型集成到形式化系统中，需要确保其生成的代码或数据（如SQL或JSON）具有语法有效性，这是处理此类输出的前提条件。

Method: 通过将大语言模型蒸馏为可处理的概率模型，并结合逻辑约束的概率进行生成引导和掩码，利用LR(k)上下文无关文法的特性实现对生成过程的约束。

Result: 证明了任何有限持续时间的LR(k)文法的满足性可以在多项式时间内计算，相较于之前方法的指数时间是一个改进。

Conclusion: 该结果使得能够高效地约束和引导大语言模型生成更符合形式句法约束的高质量输出。

Abstract: Constraining the generation of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) is an important component of integrating language models into formal systems. In the generation of code and data for tasks like program synthesis, ensuring that language models produce syntactically valid output is a prerequisite for processing such output. These languages (such as SQL or JSON) are often designed as $LR(k)$ context-free grammars. By distilling the LLM to a tractable probabilistic model, its autoregressive generation can be steered and masked to incorporate the probability of satisfying logical constraints, ensuring high quality output that is guaranteed to be valid. This paper demonstrates that the satisfaction of any $LR(k)$ grammar of finite duration can be calculated in polynomial time, an improvement over the exponential time of applying previous methods to such grammars. This result enables efficient constraint and steering of LLM generation towards output that better satisfies formal syntactic constraints.

</details>


### [223] [The Devil is in the Spectrum: Mitigating Representation Collapse in LLMs via Topologically Regularized Side-Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20484)
*Yiheng Tao,Kaiwen Cheng,Yao Lu,Chang Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TRSP的新型架构干预方法，用于解决大语言模型在长上下文处理中的表示崩溃问题，通过拓扑正则化实现注意力机制的谱平衡，在不改变核心注意力结构的情况下显著提升了长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理长上下文时面临表示崩溃的问题，现有方法容易陷入同质化或孤立化的极端情况，导致性能下降。

Method: 通过注意力动态的谱分析，揭示了混合效率与信息容量之间的内在权衡，并提出了Topologically Regularized Side-Path（TRSP）架构，结合无参数的Triangular Box机制和轻量级长度感知门控，引入近端耦合与远端传播以实现谱平衡。

Result: 实验表明TRSP在通用能力和长上下文基准测试中均有显著提升；在NoLiMa测试中，于8倍训练长度下仍保持83%准确率，分别比Differential Transformer和Gated Attention高出约30和50个百分点。

Conclusion: TRSP通过非侵入式架构设计有效解决了表示崩溃问题，在不修改核心注意力机制的前提下实现了更优的长上下文建模能力，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are fundamentally limited by representation collapse, a bottleneck that severely degrades long-context performance. We identify that existing approaches risk drifting into one of two pathological extremes: homogenization collapse (e.g., attention sinks causing rank deficiency) and isolation collapse (e.g., local attention causing context disconnection). Through spectral analysis of attention dynamics, we derive an intrinsic trade-off between mixing efficiency (spectral gap) and information capacity (effective rank) that standard mechanisms struggle to balance.
